 300      -|  #↵
 301      -|  # WHY the window: the suite-level `capture_log: true` only keeps the Logger↵
 302      -|  # :default handler swapped out WHILE a capture is active, so a log emitted↵
 303      -|  # after a test's capture closed prints to the console as uncaptured noise↵
 304      -|  # (observed in the between-test gap: `[warning] TaskRegistry:↵
 305      -|  # FAILED_TRANSITION task_id=<id> source=result_handler prev_status=:running↵
 306      -|  # result={:error, :llm_not_configured}`). with_log/1's internal Logger.flush/0↵
 307      -|  # drains pending entries before the window closes.↵
 308      -|  #↵
 309      -|  # ORDERING — awaiting the persisted terminal status is a DETERMINISTIC↵
 310      -|  # guarantee that the FAILED_TRANSITION log is inside the window: the↵
 311      -|  # registry's `{ref, result}` handler calls↵

 314      -|  # vs the ~:1489 `update_task_status_with_caller/4`), and that cast writes the↵
 315      -|  # row synchronously in `handle_update_status/6` (its ~:909 log precedes the↵
 316      -|  # ~:956 `EvoGit.Store.update_task_columns/3`). The await therefore returns↵
 317      -|  # only after the log was emitted.↵

1803      -|      File.write!(config_path, """↵
        1 +|# HomeLive (chat page) test suite.
        2 +|#
        3 +|# Covers the idle render, the send-message flow (deterministic fail-fast
        4 +|# reflect tasks against the isolated Store+TaskRegistry — see the setup's
        5 +|# "fail-fast" block: every real spawn is rejected at the AgentScheduler, so
        6 +|# no LLM endpoint is ever contacted), new-chat + ChatHistory store semantics,
        7 +|# the onboarding dead-render redirect, streaming display (REAL-shaped core
        8 +|# payloads: integer agent ids, KEYWORD-LIST changed_fields, real
        9 +|# %ReqLLM.Message{} structs), completion/error rendering, the assistant
       10 +|# task-card (status badge + thought process), chat persistence/restore across
       11 +|# remounts, the production-mimicking crash-reproduction flow, sidebar
       12 +|# robustness, stop/cancel, and node-awareness.
       13 +|#
       14 +|# The config directory is isolated PER PROCESS through the test-only
       15 +|# `:config_dir_fun` seam (`EvoDash.Test.ConfigDir.isolate!/1`, installed by
       16 +|# test/test_helper.exs), never through a BEAM-global `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` write.
       17 +|# The override lives in the TEST process' dictionary and reaches the mounted
       18 +|# LiveView plus its supervised load tasks through the OTP `:"$callers"` chain
       19 +|# (`support/config_dir_test.exs` proves all three levels), so every test gets its
       20 +|# OWN empty `<temp dir>/genesis` config dir — the exact layout the old env-var
       21 +|# redirect produced, with zero assertion changes.
       22 +|#
       23 +|# async: false — FOUR process-global dependencies, and every test in this file
       24 +|# mounts `/help`, so no contained subset can escape them:
       25 +|#
       26 +|#   1. `EvoDash.Test.IsolatedTaskStore.isolate!/1` (module-wide setup) terminates
       27 +|#      and re-registers the process-global `EvoGit.Store` / `EvoGit.TaskRegistry`
       28 +|#      SINGLETON NAMES. A (re)start cannot overlap another module's use of those
       29 +|#      names, so this alone forces the sync phase.
       30 +|#   2. `EvoDash.ChatHistory` — a boot-created global GenServer + named ETS table
       31 +|#      (`EvoDash.Application`) with NO per-process seam. This suite resets it,
       32 +|#      and the HomeLive mount itself calls `ChatHistory.new_chat/1` +
       33 +|#      `put_state/2`, while the ASYNC `EvoDash.ChatHistoryTest` asserts ABSOLUTE
       34 +|#      contents (`list_chats() == [id]`); running concurrently would make both
       35 +|#      flaky (that suite's own module comment states the same invariant).
       36 +|#   3. The boot-global `EvoGit.AgentScheduler` config is forced to the fail-fast
       37 +|#      `model_profiles: []` for every test (the documented seam that keeps a
       38 +|#      real send from reaching an LLM endpoint), then restored in `on_exit`.
       39 +|#   4. Per-test app-env seams (`:approval_responder`, `:approval_pending_runner`,
       40 +|#      `:source_availability_runner`, `:source_clone_runner`, `:config_dir_fun`)
       41 +|#      are written and restored by the tests that use them; canonical launch
       42 +|#      fixtures must also reach TaskRegistry outside the test's caller chain.
       43 +|defmodule EvoDashWeb.HomeLiveTest do
       44 +|  use EvoDashWeb.ConnCase, async: false
       45 +|  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest
       46 +|
       47 +|  alias EvoGit.TaskInfo
       48 +|
       49 +|  # Poll interval for the file's wait_until/2 helper. Tightened from 10ms to
       50 +|  # 2ms: the pollers observe async work that typically settles within a few
       51 +|  # ms, so the old interval was pure overshoot latency (the residual wait of a
       52 +|  # fixed-interval poll averages interval/2). Still non-zero so the loop never
       53 +|  # spins hot.
       54 +|  @poll_interval_ms 2
       55 +|
       56 +|  setup do
       57 +|    # Isolated Store + TaskRegistry (pattern from tasks_live_test). The helper
       58 +|    # owns teardown: it stops the isolated pair FIRST, then restores and
       59 +|    # VERIFIES the production children, so the reflect tasks started by
       60 +|    # send_message never leak into other suites and the globals are always
       61 +|    # deterministically restored afterwards.
       62 +|    :ok = EvoDash.Test.IsolatedTaskStore.isolate!("home_live")
       63 +|
       64 +|    # Fail-fast scheduler config: run_agent clause (a) (agent_scheduler.ex)
       65 +|    # rejects every spawn synchronously with {:error, :llm_not_configured} when
       66 +|    # model_profiles is [] — BEFORE any agent registration, ETS row, or LLM
       67 +|    # HTTP call. This suite is a dashboard-layer suite: the real reflect
       68 +|    # runtime (core suites cover it) must never run here. On this machine the
       69 +|    # ambient config carries a REAL (expired) API key — a live send would spam
       70 +|    # 403 network errors; on a machine with no key at all the agent crashes
       71 +|    # with a MatchError instead. The empty-profiles rejection makes every
       72 +|    # send deterministic-and-quiet in both worlds (the executor maps the
       73 +|    # {:error, _} to a persisted :failed row, with no network I/O). The same
       74 +|    # seam is the documented idiom in evo_git's own suites
       75 +|    # (evolution_test.exs without_model_profiles/1, agent_scheduler_test.exs).
       76 +|    # Per-send cleanup below still runs (the row + wrapper must be reaped);
       77 +|    # with the fail-fast in place no agent ETS rows can exist, so the
       78 +|    # cleanup's ETS sweep is a cheap no-op.
       79 +|    original_model_profiles =
       80 +|      GenServer.call(EvoGit.AgentScheduler, {:get_config, :model_profiles})
       81 +|
       82 +|    GenServer.call(EvoGit.AgentScheduler, {:update_config, model_profiles: []})
       83 +|
       84 +|    # ChatHistory is a global GenServer under EvoDash.Application that is NOT
       85 +|    # terminated by the Store/TaskRegistry isolation above — reset it so the
       86 +|    # chats persisted by one test never leak into the next (the
       87 +|    # node_aware_test.exs / chat_history_test.exs convention).
       88 +|    EvoDash.ChatHistory.reset()
       89 +|
       90 +|    # `EvoDash.ActiveTasks` is a process-less public-ETS module (NOT a GenServer) over
       91 +|    # the boot-created, process-wide `:evo_dash_active_tasks` table, so it is shared by
       92 +|    # the whole run and untouched by the `EvoDash.Test.IsolatedTaskStore.isolate!/1` above.
       93 +|    # This module is `async: false`, so its whole-table `EvoDash.ActiveTasks.reset()`
       94 +|    # cannot race another module: it clears the previous test's sidebar snapshot so it
       95 +|    # cannot seed the next whole-page mount or dead render.
       96 +|    EvoDash.ActiveTasks.reset()
       97 +|
       98 +|    # Onboarding (pattern from projects_live_test.set_onboarding_completed):
       99 +|    # isolate the config dir to a per-test temp dir and mark onboarding complete
      100 +|    # so the HomeLive dead render does NOT redirect first-time users to
      101 +|    # /welcome. The "onboarding redirect" describe re-isolates to a fresh empty
      102 +|    # dir in its own test body.
      103 +|    #
      104 +|    # `isolate!/1` creates the temp dir, activates it in THIS process and
      105 +|    # registers the `File.rm_rf!` cleanup itself; the explicit `on_exit` below
      106 +|    # keeps the dir lifetime visible at the call site (both removals are
      107 +|    # idempotent).
      108 +|    dir = EvoDash.Test.ConfigDir.isolate!("evogit_home_live_test_config")
      109 +|    on_exit(fn -> File.rm_rf!(dir) end)
      110 +|
      111 +|    if Code.ensure_loaded?(EvoGit.Config.VersionState) do
      112 +|      EvoGit.Config.VersionState.complete_onboarding()
      113 +|    end
      114 +|
      115 +|    on_exit(fn ->
      116 +|      # Restore the ambient scheduler config FIRST (before the per-test Store
      117 +|      # child dies — update_config only touches the global scheduler, and the
      118 +|      # eviction sweep in State.do_update_config reads :evogit_sched_meta via
      119 +|      # :ets.whereis, so a missing table is a safe no-op).
      120 +|      restore_model_profiles(original_model_profiles)
      121 +|    end)
      122 +|
      123 +|    :ok
      124 +|  end
      125 +|
      126 +|  # --- helpers ---
      127 +|
      128 +|  # The Phoenix.LiveViewTest View struct exposes no assigns accessor in this
      129 +|  # version, so read the LiveView socket assigns directly from the process
      130 +|  # state (same pattern as welcome_live_test.exs / settings_live_test.exs).
      131 +|  defp assigns(view), do: :sys.get_state(view.pid).socket.assigns
      132 +|
      133 +|  # Decoded task opts are a list of mixed atom/string-key tuples (the Store
      134 +|  # codec atomizes only its whitelist), so string keys must be looked up via
      135 +|  # Map.new — Access.get/3 and Keyword.has_key?/2 reject non-atom keys on
      136 +|  # keyword lists.
      137 +|  defp opt(task, key), do: Map.get(Map.new(task.opts || []), key)
      138 +|  defp has_opt?(task, key), do: Map.has_key?(Map.new(task.opts || []), key)
      139 +|
      140 +|  # Inserts a task directly into the SQLite store (bypasses the async task
      141 +|  # spawn that `start_task/2` triggers). Lets a test prove that an unrelated
      142 +|  # pre-existing row is untouched by an event.
      143 +|  #
      144 +|  # No on_exit delete is needed: the Store is per-test (setup terminates the
      145 +|  # production children and starts a fresh temp sqlite that is rm_rf'd in
      146 +|  # setup's on_exit), so rows cannot leak into other tests — and by the time
      147 +|  # an on_exit would run, the isolated Store process is already dead, so a
      148 +|  # delete would raise `(exit) no process`.
      149 +|  defp insert_task_fixture!(overrides) do
      150 +|    id = Keyword.get(overrides, :id) || "fixture_#{System.unique_integer([:positive])}"
      151 +|
      152 +|    task =
      153 +|      %TaskInfo{
      154 +|        id: id,
      155 +|        type: Keyword.get(overrides, :type, :genesis),
      156 +|        status: :completed,
      157 +|        opts: Keyword.get(overrides, :opts, path: "/tmp/test"),
      158 +|        ref: nil,
      159 +|        started_at: DateTime.utc_now(),
      160 +|        finished_at: DateTime.utc_now(),
      161 +|        logs: [],
      162 +|        result: nil
      163 +|      }
      164 +|      |> Map.merge(Enum.into(overrides, %{}))
      165 +|
      166 +|    EvoGit.Store.put_task(EvoGit.Store, task)
      167 +|
      168 +|    id
      169 +|  end
      170 +|
      171 +|  # Restores the scheduler's model_profiles captured by setup. update_config
      172 +|  # rejects a nil llm_model but accepts model_profiles lists unconditionally;
      173 +|  # the original value is whatever the ambient config resolved at boot.
      174 +|  defp restore_model_profiles(original) do
      175 +|    GenServer.call(EvoGit.AgentScheduler, {:update_config, model_profiles: original})
      176 +|  end
      177 +|
      178 +|  # Cancels + deletes a launched reflect task in on_exit so its persisted row
      179 +|  # never leaks into other tests. Under the setup's fail-fast scheduler config
      180 +|  # the wrapper already returned {:error, :llm_not_configured} → the row is
      181 +|  # terminal :failed, so the cancel call is a defensive no-op; delete is what
      182 +|  # reaps the row (only relevant for suites reading this file's leftovers,
      183 +|  # since each test's Store is rm_rf'd anyway). Additionally sweeps the
      184 +|  # scheduler ETS rows (cancel/delete do not remove them) — with the fail-fast
      185 +|  # in place no agent is ever registered, so this sweep is a cheap no-op kept
      186 +|  # for safety if the config seam is ever relaxed.
      187 +|  #
      188 +|  # Cleanup in on_exit: ExUnit terminates the test supervisor (stopping the
      189 +|  # isolated Store + TaskRegistry) BEFORE running on_exit callbacks, so the
      190 +|  # GenServer.call in cancel_task/1 exits with :noproc — catch exits so a
      191 +|  # teardown failure can't mask the actual test result (the row is also
      192 +|  # rm_rf'd by setup's on_exit regardless).
      193 +|  defp cleanup_task_on_exit(task_id) do
      194 +|    on_exit(fn ->
      195 +|      try do
      196 +|        EvoGit.TaskRegistry.cancel_task(task_id)
      197 +|      catch
      198 +|        :exit, _ -> :ok
      199 +|      end
      200 +|
      201 +|      try do
      202 +|        EvoGit.TaskRegistry.delete_task(task_id)
      203 +|      catch
      204 +|        :exit, _ -> :ok
      205 +|      end
      206 +|
      207 +|      # The reflect agent's scheduler rows (agent_id -> %{task_id: ...} in
      208 +|      # :evogit_sched_meta, plus :evogit_agent_state) are NOT removed by
      209 +|      # cancel/delete — the agent stays alive/blocked on an LLM slot with no
      210 +|      # credentials, so without this sweep its ETS rows leak into
      211 +|      # agents_live_test.exs (which expects an empty agent registry).
      212 +|      try do
      213 +|        if :ets.whereis(:evogit_sched_meta) != :undefined do
      214 +|          for {agent_id, meta} <- :ets.tab2list(:evogit_sched_meta),
      215 +|              Map.get(meta, :task_id) == task_id do
      216 +|            :ets.delete(:evogit_sched_meta, agent_id)
      217 +|
      218 +|            if :ets.whereis(:evogit_agent_state) != :undefined,
      219 +|              do: :ets.delete(:evogit_agent_state, agent_id)
      220 +|          end
      221 +|        end
      222 +|
      223 +|        :ok
      224 +|      catch
      225 +|        :exit, _ -> :ok
      226 +|      end
      227 +|    end)
      228 +|
      229 +|    task_id
      230 +|  end
      231 +|
      232 +|  # Injects a terminal `:failed` task event directly into the view (the
      233 +|  # documented test idiom for the push-based event contract — see
      234 +|  # test/CONTEXT.md "Notes for Agents") and flushes it with render/1. This
      235 +|  # deterministically drives the chat back to :idle WITHOUT asserting on the
      236 +|  # real (async) task lifecycle, which is out of scope here.
      237 +|  defp finalize_failed(view, task_id) do
      238 +|    send(view.pid, {:task_updated, task_id, :failed, node()})
      239 +|    render(view)
      240 +|  end
      241 +|
      242 +|  # Returns true when the first element matching `selector` has a `disabled`
      243 +|  # attribute in the rendered HTML (Phoenix HEEx renders `disabled={true}` as a
      244 +|  # bare attribute, omitted when false).
      245 +|  defp disabled?(html, selector) do
      246 +|    html
      247 +|    |> Floki.parse_document!()
      248 +|    |> Floki.find(selector)
      249 +|    |> case do
      250 +|      [{_tag, attrs, _children} | _] -> Enum.any?(attrs, fn {k, _v} -> k == "disabled" end)
      251 +|      _ -> false
      252 +|    end
      253 +|  end
      254 +|
      255 +|  # Returns true when at least one element matches `selector`.
      256 +|  defp present?(html, selector) do
      257 +|    html
      258 +|    |> Floki.parse_document!()
      259 +|    |> Floki.find(selector) != []
      260 +|  end
      261 +|
      262 +|  # Scope the page-alive/name pin to its heading: assistant entries and the
      263 +|  # composer also carry the assistant's name, but cannot replace the page title.
      264 +|  defp page_heading(html) do
      265 +|    html
      266 +|    |> Floki.parse_document!()
      267 +|    |> Floki.find("h1")
      268 +|    |> Floki.text()
      269 +|    |> String.trim()
      270 +|  end
      271 +|
      272 +|  # Polls `fun` until it returns truthy (or the timeout elapses) — the
      273 +|  # tasks_live_test.exs pattern for observing async results (the real
      274 +|  # supervised fetches, the PubSub debounce) without fixed sleeps. The poll
      275 +|  # interval is @poll_interval_ms (a few ms — see the attribute).
      276 +|  defp wait_until(fun, timeout \\ 2000) do
      277 +|    deadline = System.monotonic_time(:millisecond) + timeout
      278 +|
      279 +|    wait_loop = fn wait_loop ->
      280 +|      if fun.() do
      281 +|        :ok
      282 +|      else
      283 +|        if System.monotonic_time(:millisecond) >= deadline do
      284 +|          flunk("timed out waiting for async condition")
      285 +|        else
      286 +|          Process.sleep(@poll_interval_ms)
      287 +|          wait_loop.(wait_loop)
      288 +|        end
      289 +|      end
      290 +|    end
      291 +|
      292 +|    wait_loop.(wait_loop)
      293 +|  end
      294 +|
      295 +|  # Runs a REAL chat send (a typed `render_submit(view, "send_message", ...)`
      296 +|  # or a suggestion-chip `render_click`) plus an await of the started reflect
      297 +|  # task's terminal status inside ONE ExUnit.CaptureLog.with_log/2 window, and
      298 +|  # returns {html, log} — the canonical-send test's shape, shared by EVERY
      299 +|  # real-send test in this file.
      300 +|  #
      301 +|  # WHY the window: the suite-level `capture_log: true` only keeps the Logger
      302 +|  # :default handler swapped out WHILE a capture is active, so a log emitted
      303 +|  # after a test's capture closed prints to the console as uncaptured noise
      304 +|  # (observed in the between-test gap: `[warning] TaskRegistry:
      305 +|  # FAILED_TRANSITION task_id=<id> source=result_handler prev_status=:running
      306 +|  # result={:error, :llm_not_configured}`). with_log/1's internal Logger.flush/0
      307 +|  # drains pending entries before the window closes.
      308 +|  #
      309 +|  # ORDERING — awaiting the persisted terminal status is a DETERMINISTIC
      310 +|  # guarantee that the FAILED_TRANSITION log is inside the window: the
      311 +|  # registry's `{ref, result}` handler calls
      312 +|  # `Diagnostics.log_failed_transition(task_id, :result_handler, ...)` BEFORE it
      313 +|  # casts the terminal status (apps/evo_git/lib/evo_git/task_registry.ex: ~:1477
      314 +|  # vs the ~:1489 `update_task_status_with_caller/4`), and that cast writes the
      315 +|  # row synchronously in `handle_update_status/6` (its ~:909 log precedes the
      316 +|  # ~:956 `EvoGit.Store.update_task_columns/3`). The await therefore returns
      317 +|  # only after the log was emitted.
      318 +|  #
      319 +|  # TOTAL await: `TaskRegistry.start_task/2` is a GenServer.call, so a real
      320 +|  # LOCAL send has already persisted its row (non-terminal) by the time
      321 +|  # `send_fun` returns — the poll then waits it to terminal. A send that starts
      322 +|  # NO task at all (a gate-blocked composer, or a remote fail-fast send that
      323 +|  # persists no local row) leaves no non-terminal :reflect row, so the poll is
      324 +|  # immediately satisfied.
      325 +|  defp send_and_await_terminal(send_fun) when is_function(send_fun, 0) do
      326 +|    ExUnit.CaptureLog.with_log(fn ->
      327 +|      html = send_fun.()
      328 +|      await_reflect_terminal()
      329 +|      html
      330 +|    end)
      331 +|  end
      332 +|
      333 +|  # Polls until every :reflect row in the store is terminal (an empty set counts
      334 +|  # as settled). The file's own wait_until/2 poll idiom — no fixed sleep.
      335 +|  defp await_reflect_terminal do
      336 +|    wait_until(fn ->
      337 +|      EvoGit.Store.safe_select_all_tasks(EvoGit.Store)
      338 +|      |> Enum.filter(&(&1.type == :reflect))
      339 +|      |> Enum.all?(&(&1.status in [:failed, :completed, :cancelled]))
      340 +|    end)
      341 +|  end
      342 +|
      343 +|  # Polls until the async Genesis-source availability check has landed on the
      344 +|  # socket (its runner resolves at spawn time inside a TaskSupervisor child, so
      345 +|  # render_async/2 cannot await it — same reason the file polls other async
      346 +|  # assigns) and then syncs the test proxy via render/1.
      347 +|  defp await_source_available(view, expected, timeout \\ 2000) do
      348 +|    wait_until(fn -> assigns(view)[:source_available] == expected end, timeout)
      349 +|    render(view)
      350 +|  end
      351 +|
      352 +|  # Restores an Application env key captured before a test mutated it (a stored
      353 +|  # `false`/`nil` is handled correctly). Mirrors system_live_test.exs'
      354 +|  # restore_env_value/2.
      355 +|  defp restore_env_value(key, original) do
      356 +|    if original != nil do
      357 +|      Application.put_env(:evo_dash, key, original)
      358 +|    else
      359 +|      Application.delete_env(:evo_dash, key)
      360 +|    end
      361 +|  end
      362 +|
      363 +|  # A REAL-shaped history payload (the exact production data path): native
      364 +|  # %ReqLLM.Message{} structs with a :thinking content part, tool_calls,
      365 +|  # reasoning_details, a tool message, and a nil-metadata message.
      366 +|  defp real_history do
      367 +|    [
      368 +|      %ReqLLM.Message{
      369 +|        role: :system,
      370 +|        content: [%ReqLLM.Message.ContentPart{type: :text, text: "you are genesis"}],

      372 +|      },

      374 +|        role: :user,
      375 +|        content: [%ReqLLM.Message.ContentPart{type: :text, text: "New message: hi"}],
      376 +|        metadata: %{turn: 1, timestamp: 1_700_000_001}
      377 +|      },
      378 +|      %ReqLLM.Message{

      405 +|        ],

      407 +|      }

      409 +|  end
      410 +|
      411 +|  # Builds a %ReqLLM.Context{} from the real history (for seeding REAL
      412 +|  # :evogit_agent_state rows).
      413 +|  defp real_context, do: ReqLLM.Context.new(real_history())
      414 +|
      415 +|  describe "render" do
      416 +|    test "renders the idle chat page", %{conn: conn} do
      417 +|      {:ok, _view, html} = live(conn, "/help")
      418 +|
      419 +|      assert page_heading(html) == "Genesis Assistant"
      420 +|      assert html =~ "Start a conversation"
      421 +|
      422 +|      # The message input and Send button are present and ENABLED on idle
      423 +|      # (chat_status == :idle → disabled={false} → attribute omitted).
      424 +|      assert present?(html, ~s(textarea[name="message"]))
      425 +|      refute disabled?(html, ~s(textarea[name="message"]))
      426 +|      assert present?(html, ~s(button[type="submit"]))
      427 +|      refute disabled?(html, ~s(button[type="submit"]))
      428 +|
      429 +|      # New chat is enabled on idle (it is disabled only while a chat is
      430 +|      # running); Stop is disabled when nothing is running.
      431 +|      assert present?(html, ~s(button[phx-click="new_chat"]))
      432 +|      refute disabled?(html, ~s(button[phx-click="new_chat"]))
      433 +|      assert present?(html, ~s(button[phx-click="stop"]))
      434 +|      assert disabled?(html, ~s(button[phx-click="stop"]))
      435 +|    end
      436 +|
      437 +|    test "empty state renders the greeting and four operator suggestion chips", %{conn: conn} do
      438 +|      {:ok, _view, html} = live(conn, "/help")
      439 +|
      440 +|      # ChatGPT-style empty state: greeting + 4 suggestion chips, each wired to
      441 +|      # the send_message event with its message text as the phx-value (so
      442 +|      # clicking a chip submits it through the existing handle_event clause).
      443 +|      assert html =~ "What would you like Genesis to do?"
      444 +|
      445 +|      chips =
      446 +|        html

      449 +|

      454 +|      assert html =~ ~s(phx-value-message="Start a task for me")
      455 +|      assert html =~ ~s(phx-value-message="Explain how tasks are scheduled")
      456 +|    end
      457 +|  end
      458 +|
      459 +|  # Extracts the FIRST chat-entry wrapper + its bubble's class attribute from
      460 +|  # rendered html. The user message is always the first transcript entry after a
      461 +|  # fresh send (document order), and its wrapper is right-aligned
      462 +|  # (`flex justify-end`) around a single content-fit bubble div (the assistant
      463 +|  # entries follow with `flex justify-start` wrappers).
      464 +|  defp first_user_bubble(html) do
      465 +|    doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
      466 +|
      467 +|    wrapper_class =
      468 +|      doc
      469 +|      |> Floki.find(~s(div[id^="chat-entry-"]))
      470 +|      |> hd()
      471 +|      |> Floki.attribute("class")
      472 +|      |> List.first()
      473 +|
      474 +|    bubble_class =
      475 +|      doc
      476 +|      |> Floki.find(~s(div[id^="chat-entry-"] > div))
      477 +|      |> hd()
      478 +|      |> Floki.attribute("class")
      479 +|      |> List.first()
      480 +|
      481 +|    {wrapper_class, bubble_class}
      482 +|  end
      483 +|
      484 +|  describe "send message" do
      485 +|    # THE canonical real-send pin (every other send in this file is deduped
      486 +|    # against it or the model-selector describes). Under the setup's fail-fast
      487 +|    # scheduler config the reflect wrapper returns {:error, :llm_not_configured}
      488 +|    # deterministically — the row + its opts land on the persisted TaskInfo, no
      489 +|    # agent is registered, and no LLM endpoint is ever contacted. The submit +
      490 +|    # terminal await run inside a CaptureLog.with_log/2 window so the wrapper's
      491 +|    # asynchronous error log is captured rather than printed between tests.
      492 +|    test "starts a reflect task with the right opts and tracks it synchronously", %{conn: conn} do
      493 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      494 +|
      495 +|      # The wrapper's `SelfReflective failed: ...` error log is emitted
      496 +|      # ASYNCHRONOUSLY at task end, so the submit + the await of the row's
      497 +|      # terminal status run inside ONE ExUnit.CaptureLog.with_log/2 window (see
      498 +|      # send_and_await_terminal/1, which documents the ordering guarantee and
      499 +|      # why an un-windowed send prints uncaptured noise between tests).
      500 +|      {html, log} =
      501 +|        send_and_await_terminal(fn ->
      502 +|          render_submit(view, "send_message", %{"message" => "hello genesis"})
      503 +|        end)
      504 +|
      505 +|      # Deterministic under the fail-fast pin — proof the capture window really
      506 +|      # held the wrapper's error log (it is never printed to the console).
      507 +|      assert log =~ "SelfReflective failed"
      508 +|      # Optimistic UI: the user bubble appears immediately and the start flow
      509 +|      # did not render the error bubble. (Regression: passing the bare
      510 +|      # %EvoGit.TaskInfo{} struct — instead of {:ok, task} — to
      511 +|      # AgentStream.task_id_from_start/1 fell through to {:error, :no_task_id},
      512 +|      # leaving chat_task_id nil and rendering the error bubble even though the
      513 +|      # reflect task DID start.)
      514 +|      assert html =~ "hello genesis"
      515 +|      refute html =~ "Failed to start the task"
      516 +|
      517 +|      # The persisted row: repo-less reflect task with the FIRST message as the
      518 +|      # bare objective, mode "reflect", and NO :path key (atom or string).
      519 +|      tasks = EvoGit.Store.safe_select_all_tasks(EvoGit.Store)
      520 +|      reflect = Enum.filter(tasks, &(&1.type == :reflect))
      521 +|      assert length(reflect) == 1
      522 +|      task = hd(reflect)
      523 +|      assert opt(task, :mode) == "reflect"
      524 +|      assert opt(task, :objective) == "hello genesis"
      525 +|      assert opt(task, :path) == nil
      526 +|      refute has_opt?(task, :path)
      527 +|      refute has_opt?(task, "path")
      528 +|
      529 +|      # Synchronous tracking, proved race-free: a :failed event for the ROW's
      530 +|      # id only finalizes the transcript when the chat is tracking exactly
      531 +|      # that id (handle_task_event drops non-matching ids). The wrapper may
      532 +|      # already have broadcast the real :failed (the finalize is idempotent),
      533 +|      # but a chat that failed to track its task would stay :running with a
      534 +|      # still-streaming bubble.
      535 +|      finalize_failed(view, task.id)
      536 +|
      537 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
      538 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
      539 +|      assert assigns(view).transcript |> List.last() |> Map.get(:text) == "The task failed."
      540 +|
      541 +|      cleanup_task_on_exit(task.id)
      542 +|    end
      543 +|
      544 +|    test "second message carries the transcript preamble", %{conn: conn} do
      545 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      546 +|
      547 +|      # Establish the transcript deterministically via :sys.replace_state (the
      548 +|      # real reflect-task lifecycle is async and out of scope — see the
      549 +|      # "completion / error rendering" describes for the full recipe). The
      550 +|      # socket assigns are mutated directly (same pattern as
      551 +|      # agents_live_test.exs) — Phoenix.LiveView.assign/2 does not exist.
      552 +|      :sys.replace_state(view.pid, fn state ->
      553 +|        %{
      554 +|          state
      555 +|          | socket: %{
      556 +|              state.socket
      557 +|              | assigns:
      558 +|                  Map.merge(state.socket.assigns, %{
      559 +|                    transcript: [%{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false}],
      560 +|                    chat_status: :idle
      561 +|                  })
      562 +|            }
      563 +|        }
      564 +|      end)
      565 +|
      566 +|      {_html, log} =
      567 +|        send_and_await_terminal(fn ->
      568 +|          render_submit(view, "send_message", %{"message" => "what is genesis?"})
      569 +|        end)
      570 +|
      571 +|      # Deterministic proof that the capture window really held the registry's
      572 +|      # asynchronous FAILED_TRANSITION warning: the persisted row only reaches
      573 +|      # its terminal status AFTER that log is emitted (see
      574 +|      # send_and_await_terminal/1), so a log landing between tests would make
      575 +|      # this assert flunk instead of leaking to the console.
      576 +|      assert log =~ "FAILED_TRANSITION"
      577 +|
      578 +|      # The persisted row's objective must carry the preamble.
      579 +|      [task] =
      580 +|        EvoGit.Store.safe_select_all_tasks(EvoGit.Store)
      581 +|        |> Enum.filter(&(&1.type == :reflect))
      582 +|
      583 +|      assert opt(task, :objective) ==
      584 +|               "Previous conversation:\nUser: hello\nNew message: what is genesis?"
      585 +|
      586 +|      cleanup_task_on_exit(task.id)
      587 +|    end
      588 +|
      589 +|    test "whitespace-only message is a no-op", %{conn: conn} do

      605 +|    # The user-bubble markup is identical on the typed and chip send routes

      607 +|    # pins the classes against a SEEDED transcript materialized by a real

      609 +|    # handle_task_event's assign/persist path) — no task needs to start. The

      611 +|    # "model selector" chip tests below.

      619 +|      })

      622 +|      # assign path (replace_state alone never pushes a diff — see the badge

      643 +|  describe "new chat" do
      644 +|    test "resets the chat to the idle empty state", %{conn: conn} do
      645 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      646 +|
      647 +|      # Synthetic running chat (seed + terminal event) — the real reflect
      648 +|      # runtime must never run in this suite (see the setup's fail-fast
      649 +|      # block). The seeded "hello" user entry gives new_chat a non-empty
      650 +|      # transcript to reset, exactly like a real send would.
      651 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
      652 +|
      653 +|      # Deterministically drive the chat to a terminal state via the same
      654 +|      # {:task_updated, ...} event shape the core broadcasts.
      655 +|      finalize_failed(view, "t1")
      656 +|
      657 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
      658 +|
      659 +|      html = render_click(view, "new_chat", %{})
      660 +|      assert html =~ "Start a conversation"

      662 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
      663 +|      refute disabled?(html, ~s(button[type="submit"]))
      664 +|    end
      665 +|
      666 +|    test "starts a fresh persisted chat and keeps the old one in the store", %{conn: conn} do
      667 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      668 +|      old_id = assigns(view).chat_id
      669 +|      assert old_id != nil
      670 +|
      671 +|      # Synthetic running chat (seed + terminal event) — same rationale as the
      672 +|      # "resets the chat" test above.
      673 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
      674 +|
      675 +|      # Deterministically drive the chat to a terminal state (same event
      676 +|      # shape as the reset test above).
      677 +|      finalize_failed(view, "t1")
      678 +|
      679 +|      old_state = EvoDash.ChatHistory.get_state(old_id)
      680 +|      assert old_state != nil
      681 +|      assert Enum.any?(old_state.transcript, &(&1.role == :user and &1.text == "hello"))
      682 +|
      683 +|      html = render_click(view, "new_chat", %{})
      684 +|      new_id = assigns(view).chat_id
      685 +|
      686 +|      assert new_id != nil and new_id != old_id
      687 +|      assert html =~ "Start a conversation"
      688 +|      assert assigns(view).transcript == []
      689 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
      690 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
      691 +|
      692 +|      # The store keeps the old chat AND points the current pointer at the new
      693 +|      # one (old chats are kept — there is no chat-switching UI yet).
      694 +|      assert EvoDash.ChatHistory.current_chat_id() == new_id
      695 +|      assert old_id in EvoDash.ChatHistory.list_chats()
      696 +|      assert EvoDash.ChatHistory.get_state(old_id) == old_state
      697 +|    end
      698 +|
      699 +|    test "new chat prunes the store to the newest 10 chats", %{conn: conn} do

      718 +|  describe "onboarding redirect" do
      719 +|    test "dead render redirects first-time users to /welcome", %{conn: conn} do
      720 +|      # The setup completed onboarding under ITS temp config dir. Re-isolate to
      721 +|      # a brand-new empty temp dir here (a SECOND `isolate!/1` — it returns a
      722 +|      # fresh directory and re-activates this process) so VersionState's
      723 +|      # path-keyed :persistent_term cache re-reads from disk, finds no
      724 +|      # version-state file, and reports onboarding_needed?() == true again.
      725 +|      dir = EvoDash.Test.ConfigDir.isolate!("evogit_home_live_onboarding")
      726 +|      on_exit(fn -> File.rm_rf!(dir) end)
      727 +|
      728 +|      assert {:error, {:live_redirect, %{to: "/welcome"}}} = live(conn, "/help")
      729 +|    end
      730 +|  end
      731 +|
      732 +|  # Establishes deterministic chat state by mutating the LiveView's socket
      733 +|  # assigns directly (the agents_live_test.exs pattern — Phoenix.LiveView.assign
      734 +|  # does not exist in this LiveView version). The keys are only marked changed
      735 +|  # (and a diff pushed) when a subsequent event handler runs assign/2, so
      736 +|  # rendered-html assertions are reliable only AFTER sending a message that
      737 +|  # triggers an assign; assigns/1 (sys.get_state) is always accurate for state
      738 +|  # assertions.
      739 +|  defp seed_chat_state(view, overrides) do
      740 +|    :sys.replace_state(view.pid, fn state ->
      741 +|      %{state | socket: %{state.socket | assigns: Map.merge(state.socket.assigns, overrides)}}
      742 +|    end)
      743 +|  end
      744 +|
      745 +|  # A running chat: a user bubble + an empty streaming assistant bubble (the
      746 +|  # optimistic state right after send_message), with the task/agent refs set.
      747 +|  defp seed_running_chat(view) do
      748 +|    seed_chat_state(view, %{
      749 +|      chat_status: :running,
      750 +|      chat_task_id: "t1",
      751 +|      chat_agent_id: 1001,
      752 +|      transcript: [
      753 +|        %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
      754 +|        %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
      755 +|      ]
      756 +|    })
      757 +|  end
      758 +|
      759 +|  # Seeds a REAL %AgentState{} row into the scheduler ETS (the exact shape the
      760 +|  # core keeps) and sweeps it in on_exit.
      761 +|  defp seed_agent_state!(agent_id, context \\ real_context()) do
      762 +|    state = %EvoGit.AgentScheduler.AgentState{
      763 +|      context: context,
      764 +|      context_node: %EvoGit.Core.ContextNode{path: "/tmp/x", repo: "/tmp/x"},
      765 +|      llm_model: nil,
      766 +|      max_retries: 1,
      767 +|      max_depth: 1,
      768 +|      turn: 4,
      769 +|      task_local_id: 9
      770 +|    }
      771 +|
      772 +|    :ets.insert(:evogit_agent_state, {agent_id, state})
      773 +|
      774 +|    on_exit(fn ->
      775 +|      :ets.delete(:evogit_agent_state, agent_id)
      776 +|    end)
      777 +|
      778 +|    state
      779 +|  end
      780 +|
      781 +|  describe "streaming display" do
      782 +|    test "assistant text from the history fetch appears in the bubble", %{conn: conn} do
      783 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      784 +|
      785 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
      786 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
      787 +|
      788 +|      send(
      789 +|        view.pid,
      790 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, node(), seq, 1001,
      791 +|         [%{role: :assistant, content: [%{text: "Genesis responds"}]}]}
      792 +|      )
      793 +|

      795 +|      assert html =~ "Genesis responds"
      796 +|      assert assigns(view).agent_message_count == 1
      797 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :running
      798 +|    end
      799 +|
      800 +|    test "agent_registered sets the chat agent and triggers the history fetch", %{conn: conn} do
      801 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      802 +|
      803 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
      804 +|        chat_status: :running,
      805 +|        chat_task_id: "t1",
      806 +|        chat_agent_id: nil,
      807 +|        transcript: [
      808 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
      809 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
      810 +|        ]
      811 +|      })
      812 +|
      813 +|      send(
      814 +|        view.pid,
      815 +|        {:agent_registered, 1001,

      817 +|      )
      818 +|
      819 +|      render(view)
      820 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_agent_id == 1001
      821 +|
      822 +|      # agent_registered spawned a REAL async history fetch (bumping
      823 +|      # chat_fetch_seq); it returns [] for the fake agent id, which is harmless.
      824 +|      # Inject the manual result with the CURRENT seq (read dynamically — never
      825 +|      # hardcode).
      826 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
      827 +|
      828 +|      send(
      829 +|        view.pid,
      830 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, node(), seq, 1001,
      831 +|         [%{role: :assistant, content: [%{text: "Genesis responds"}]}]}
      832 +|      )
      833 +|
      834 +|      html = render(view)
      835 +|      assert html =~ "Genesis responds"
      836 +|    end
      837 +|
      838 +|    test "kwlist changed_fields with :message_count triggers a history refetch (regression)", %{
      839 +|      conn: conn
      840 +|    } do
      841 +|      # The PROD BUG this pins: the core broadcasts changed_fields as a
      842 +|      # KEYWORD LIST (real shape [message_count: n]) — the old
      843 +|      # tuple-membership guard was always false for it, so the streamed text
      844 +|      # never updated after the first history fetch.
      845 +|      agent_id = 200_000 + rem(System.unique_integer([:positive]), 50_000)
      846 +|
      847 +|      ctx =
      848 +|        ReqLLM.Context.new([
      849 +|          %ReqLLM.Message{
      850 +|            role: :assistant,
      851 +|            content: [%ReqLLM.Message.ContentPart{type: :text, text: "kwlist response"}]
      852 +|          }
      853 +|        ])
      854 +|
      855 +|      seed_agent_state!(agent_id, ctx)
      856 +|
      857 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      858 +|
      859 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
      860 +|
      861 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
      862 +|        chat_status: :running,
      863 +|        chat_task_id: "t1",
      864 +|        chat_agent_id: agent_id,
      865 +|        transcript: [
      866 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
      867 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
      868 +|        ]
      869 +|      })
      870 +|
      871 +|      # Real keyword-list changed_fields: the guard (is_list +
      872 +|      # Keyword.has_key?) matches → a REAL refetch is spawned (seq bumped).
      873 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, agent_id, [message_count: 2], node()})
      874 +|      render(view)
      875 +|      assert assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq] == seq + 1
      876 +|
      877 +|      # The real refetch lands against the ETS row → the streamed text appears
      878 +|      # (this is the exact production data path, no injected result).
      879 +|      wait_until(fn -> assigns(view)[:agent_message_count] == 1 end)
      880 +|      html = render(view)
      881 +|      assert html =~ "kwlist response"
      882 +|    end
      883 +|
      884 +|    test "kwlist changed_fields WITHOUT :message_count does not refetch (HistoryGate)", %{
      885 +|      conn: conn
      886 +|    } do
      887 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      888 +|
      889 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
      890 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
      891 +|
      892 +|      # Other field changes (status/turn/...) must NOT refetch the history.
      893 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, 1001, [status: :running, turn: 2], node()})
      894 +|      render(view)
      895 +|
      896 +|      assert assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq] == seq
      897 +|    end
      898 +|
      899 +|    test "atom-list changed_fields is ignored (the core sends kwlists, never atom lists)", %{
      900 +|      conn: conn
      901 +|    } do
      902 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      903 +|
      904 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
      905 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
      906 +|
      907 +|      # A legacy atom list is NOT a keyword list → Keyword.has_key? rejects it
      908 +|      # (pin: only [message_count: n] triggers a refetch).
      909 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, 1001, [:message_count], node()})
      910 +|      render(view)
      911 +|
      912 +|      assert assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq] == seq
      913 +|    end
      914 +|
      915 +|    test "real-shaped core event sequence does not crash", %{conn: conn} do
      916 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
      917 +|
      918 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
      919 +|        chat_status: :running,
      920 +|        chat_task_id: "t1",
      921 +|        chat_agent_id: nil,
      922 +|        transcript: [
      923 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
      924 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
      925 +|        ]
      926 +|      })
      927 +|
      928 +|      # Real order from the core: agent_updated FIRST (agent-state insert, no
      929 +|      # message_count), then the throttled agents_updated, then
      930 +|      # agent_registered with an INTEGER id.
      931 +|      send(
      932 +|        view.pid,
      933 +|        {:agent_updated, 1, [total_tokens: 0, compression_count: 0, objective: "hi"], node()}
      934 +|      )
      935 +|
      936 +|      send(view.pid, {:agents_updated, node()})
      937 +|      render(view)
      938 +|
      939 +|      send(
      940 +|        view.pid,
      941 +|        {:agent_registered, 1,
      942 +|         %{
      943 +|           status: :pending,
      944 +|           depth: 0,
      945 +|           parent_id: nil,
      946 +|           task_id: "t1",
      947 +|           task_number: 1,
      948 +|           objective: "hi"
      949 +|         }, node()}
      950 +|      )
      951 +|
      952 +|      render(view)
      953 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_agent_id == 1
      954 +|
      955 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, 1, [status: :running, worktree: "/tmp/x"], node()})
      956 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, 1, [turn: 2], node()})
      957 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, 1, [message_count: 3], node()})
      958 +|      render(view)
      959 +|
      960 +|      # Inject the fetched history with the CURRENT seq (the kwlist
      961 +|      # :message_count refetch bumped it).
      962 +|      send(
      963 +|        view.pid,
      964 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, node(), assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq], 1, real_history()}
      965 +|      )
      966 +|
      967 +|      # Await the assign the {:chat_history_loaded, ...} handler writes (the
      968 +|      # same barrier the sibling "real AgentState ETS row" test uses) so the
      969 +|      # render below reads the fully-applied injected history.
      970 +|      wait_until(fn -> assigns(view)[:agent_message_count] == length(real_history()) end)
      971 +|
      972 +|      html = render(view)
      973 +|      assert html =~ "Genesis is an Elixir framework."
      974 +|      assert html =~ "Thought process"
      975 +|
      976 +|      # Double agent_removed (the core's recycling emits TWO) — no-op.
      977 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_removed, 1, node()})
      978 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_removed, 1, node()})
      979 +|
      980 +|      html = render(view)
      981 +|      assert page_heading(html) == "Genesis Assistant"
      982 +|    end
      983 +|
      984 +|    test "real AgentState ETS row flows through the real get_agent_history path", %{conn: conn} do
      985 +|      agent_id = 300_000 + rem(System.unique_integer([:positive]), 50_000)
      986 +|      seed_agent_state!(agent_id)
      987 +|
      988 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")

      994 +|        transcript: [

      999 +|
     1000 +|      send(
     1001 +|        view.pid,

     1009 +|           objective: "hi"
     1010 +|         }, node()}
     1011 +|      )
     1012 +|
     1013 +|      # The REAL async fetch (NodeContext → RemoteNode → RemoteAPI
     1014 +|      # .get_agent_history) reads the ETS row and returns the native
     1015 +|      # %ReqLLM.Message{} structs — nil metadata, thinking parts, tool_calls

     1024 +|
     1025 +|    test "foreign-node events are ignored", %{conn: conn} do
     1026 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1027 +|
     1028 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
     1029 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
     1030 +|
     1031 +|      send(
     1032 +|        view.pid,
     1033 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, :other@host, seq, 1001,
     1034 +|         [%{role: :assistant, content: [%{text: "NOPE"}]}]}
     1035 +|      )
     1036 +|
     1037 +|      send(view.pid, {:agent_updated, 1001, [message_count: 1], :other@host})
     1038 +|
     1039 +|      send(
     1040 +|        view.pid,
     1041 +|        {:agent_registered, 1009, %{task_id: "t1", message_count: 1}, :other@host}
     1042 +|      )
     1043 +|
     1044 +|      html = render(view)
     1045 +|      refute html =~ "NOPE"
     1046 +|
     1047 +|      # Transcript unchanged: user bubble + still-streaming empty assistant
     1048 +|      # bubble; the foreign agent_registered never set chat_agent_id.
     1049 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_agent_id == 1001
     1050 +|      assert assigns(view).agent_message_count == nil
     1051 +|
     1052 +|      assert [%{role: :user, text: "hello"}, %{role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}] =
     1053 +|               assigns(view).transcript
     1054 +|    end
     1055 +|
     1056 +|    test "streamed text stays plain (never markdown) with no action group", %{conn: conn} do
     1057 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1058 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
     1059 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
     1060 +|
     1061 +|      # The history fetch replaces the in-progress bubble text (streaming stays
     1062 +|      # true) — a mid-stream render must show the literal source + caret, never
     1063 +|      # a markdown render (half-rendered streams would flicker).
     1064 +|      send(
     1065 +|        view.pid,
     1066 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, node(), seq, 1001,
     1067 +|         [%{role: :assistant, content: [%{text: "partial **md**"}]}]}
     1068 +|      )
     1069 +|

     1071 +|      assert html =~ "partial **md**"
     1072 +|      assert html =~ "help-caret"
     1073 +|      refute html =~ "md-content"
     1074 +|      # No hover action group on the still-streaming entry.
     1075 +|      refute html =~ "toggle_assistant_raw"
     1076 +|      refute html =~ "assistant-copy-"
     1077 +|
     1078 +|      assert [%{role: :user}, %{role: :assistant, text: "partial **md**", streaming: true}] =
     1079 +|               assigns(view).transcript
     1080 +|    end
     1081 +|  end
     1082 +|
     1083 +|  # Counts the assistant card's "Task" badge headers in rendered html. The
     1084 +|  # regex requires the text to be the WHOLE element content, so the sidebar
     1085 +|  # heading "Active Tasks" (more text inside one element) never counts.
     1086 +|  defp badge_count(html), do: length(Regex.scan(~r/>\s*Task\s*</s, html))
     1087 +|
     1088 +|  describe "completion / error rendering" do

     1090 +|    # with the CURRENT chat_task_fetch_seq (read dynamically). The handler only

     1228 +|    test "failed task whose error record carries a message renders that message", %{conn: conn} do
     1229 +|      # The fetched-task :failed terminal path (finalize_terminal → finalize_failed):
     1230 +|      # the decoded TaskInfo error record (atom-keyed %{kind:, source:, message:,
     1231 +|      # stacktrace:}) drives the final text via
     1232 +|      # AgentStream.extract_failed_message/1.
     1233 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1234 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_task_fetch_seq]
     1235 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
     1236 +|
     1237 +|      send(
     1238 +|        view.pid,
     1239 +|        {:chat_task_loaded, node(), seq, "t1",
     1240 +|         %{
     1241 +|           status: :failed,
     1242 +|           error: %{
     1243 +|             kind: :force_kill,
     1244 +|             source: :force_kill_task,
     1245 +|             message: "Task force-killed by user",
     1246 +|             stacktrace: nil
     1247 +|           }
     1248 +|         }}
     1249 +|      )
     1250 +|
     1251 +|      html = render(view)
     1252 +|      assert html =~ "Task force-killed by user"
     1253 +|      # The structured message REPLACES the generic placeholder.
     1254 +|      refute html =~ "The task failed."
     1255 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     1256 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
     1257 +|      # clear_task_refs keeps the :failed badge on the final assistant card.
     1258 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_task_status == :failed
     1259 +|
     1260 +|      assert [
     1261 +|               %{role: :user, text: "hello"},
     1262 +|               %{role: :assistant, text: "Task force-killed by user", streaming: false}
     1263 +|             ] = assigns(view).transcript
     1264 +|    end
     1265 +|
     1266 +|    test "failed task without a usable error message falls back to The task failed", %{conn: conn} do
     1267 +|      # error absent / nil / empty / blank-only message — extract_failed_message/1
     1268 +|      # returns nil for each, so finalize_failed shows the generic gettext
     1269 +|      # fallback. Each payload needs its own view (one terminal injection per
     1270 +|      # chat), so iterate with a fresh mount per payload.
     1271 +|      payloads = [
     1272 +|        %{status: :failed},
     1273 +|        %{status: :failed, error: nil},
     1274 +|        %{status: :failed, error: %{kind: :boom, message: ""}},
     1275 +|        %{status: :failed, error: %{kind: :boom, message: "   "}}
     1276 +|      ]
     1277 +|
     1278 +|      for payload <- payloads do
     1279 +|        {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1280 +|        seq = assigns(view)[:chat_task_fetch_seq]
     1281 +|        seed_running_chat(view)
     1282 +|        send(view.pid, {:chat_task_loaded, node(), seq, "t1", payload})
     1283 +|        html = render(view)
     1284 +|        assert html =~ "The task failed.", "expected the fallback for #{inspect(payload)}"
     1285 +|        assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     1286 +|        assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
     1287 +|        GenServer.stop(view.pid)
     1288 +|      end
     1289 +|    end
     1290 +|
     1291 +|    test "legacy failed rows without a structured error render the fallback, no crash", %{
     1292 +|      conn: conn
     1293 +|    } do
     1294 +|      # Pre-feature failed rows carry no decoded error field (only the legacy
     1295 +|      # {:error, _} result tuple) — and an error value that is not a map must
     1296 +|      # degrade exactly like an absent one. extract_failed_message/1 never
     1297 +|      # raises, so each payload finalizes to the generic placeholder.
     1298 +|      payloads = [
     1299 +|        # Legacy row: result {:error, reason}, NO error key.
     1300 +|        %{status: :failed, result: {:error, :boom}},
     1301 +|        # error key present but the value is not a map.
     1302 +|        %{status: :failed, error: {:error, :boom}},
     1303 +|        %{status: :failed, error: "legacy string error"}
     1304 +|      ]
     1305 +|
     1306 +|      for payload <- payloads do
     1307 +|        {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1308 +|        seq = assigns(view)[:chat_task_fetch_seq]
     1309 +|        seed_running_chat(view)
     1310 +|        send(view.pid, {:chat_task_loaded, node(), seq, "t1", payload})
     1311 +|        html = render(view)
     1312 +|        assert html =~ "The task failed.", "expected the fallback for #{inspect(payload)}"
     1313 +|        assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     1314 +|        assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
     1315 +|        assert assigns(view).chat_task_status == :failed
     1316 +|        GenServer.stop(view.pid)
     1317 +|      end
     1318 +|    end
     1319 +|

     1350 +|    test "reflect task result renders as a mini task-card chat message", %{conn: conn} do

     1408 +|  describe "assistant task-card" do
     1409 +|    test "status badge attaches to the last assistant entry and tracks task events", %{
     1410 +|      conn: conn
     1411 +|    } do
     1412 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1413 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
     1414 +|      # Before any task event there is no badge (chat_task_status nil). Scoped
     1415 +|      # to .badge elements: the run-command approvals HTML comment in
     1416 +|      # home_live.ex also contains the word "Pending", so a bare substring
     1417 +|      # refute would always fail — the badge's presence is what matters.
     1418 +|      html = render(view)
     1419 +|      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
     1420 +|      assert Floki.find(doc, ".badge") == []
     1421 +|      # :pending → "Pending" badge appears on the card header.
     1422 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t1", :pending, node()})
     1423 +|      html = render(view)
     1424 +|      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
     1425 +|      assert [badge] = Floki.find(doc, ".badge")
     1426 +|      assert Floki.text(badge) =~ "Pending"
     1427 +|      assert badge_count(html) == 1
     1428 +|      # :running → "Running" + the pulsing-dot convention (animate-ping).
     1429 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t1", :running, node()})
     1430 +|      html = render(view)
     1431 +|      assert html =~ "Running"
     1432 +|      assert html =~ "animate-ping"
     1433 +|      # :completed → async_fetch_task bumped the seq; inject the terminal
     1434 +|      # result deterministically with the CURRENT seq.
     1435 +|      send(
     1436 +|        view.pid,
     1437 +|        {:chat_task_loaded, node(), assigns(view)[:chat_task_fetch_seq], "t1",
     1438 +|         %{status: :completed, result: {:ok, %{result: "done at last"}}}}
     1439 +|      )
     1440 +|
     1441 +|      html = render(view)
     1442 +|      assert html =~ "done at last"
     1443 +|      assert html =~ "Completed"
     1444 +|      assert badge_count(html) == 1
     1445 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     1446 +|    end
     1447 +|
     1448 +|    test "only the LAST assistant entry carries the badge", %{conn: conn} do
     1449 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1450 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
     1451 +|
     1452 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
     1453 +|        chat_status: :running,
     1454 +|        chat_task_id: "t1",
     1455 +|        chat_agent_id: 1001,
     1456 +|        transcript: [
     1457 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
     1458 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "older answer", streaming: false},
     1459 +|          %{id: "3", role: :user, text: "again", streaming: false},
     1460 +|          %{id: "4", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     1461 +|        ]
     1462 +|      })
     1463 +|
     1464 +|      # Materialize the seeded transcript into the rendered html via a real
     1465 +|      # assign path (history fetch updates the streaming bubble).
     1466 +|      send(
     1467 +|        view.pid,
     1468 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, node(), seq, 1001,
     1469 +|         [%{role: :assistant, content: [%{text: "streamed"}]}]}
     1470 +|      )
     1471 +|
     1472 +|      html = render(view)
     1473 +|      assert html =~ "older answer"
     1474 +|      assert html =~ "streamed"
     1475 +|      # No badge yet (chat_task_status nil).
     1476 +|      assert badge_count(html) == 0
     1477 +|      # A task event flips the badge on — but ONLY on the LAST assistant
     1478 +|      # entry (index 3), never the earlier one.
     1479 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t1", :running, node()})
     1480 +|      html = render(view)
     1481 +|      assert html =~ "Running"
     1482 +|      assert badge_count(html) == 1
     1483 +|    end
     1484 +|
     1485 +|    test "thought process section lists context-history entries incl. tool calls", %{
     1486 +|      conn: conn
     1487 +|    } do
     1488 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1489 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
     1490 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
     1491 +|      send(view.pid, {:chat_history_loaded, node(), seq, 1001, real_history()})
     1492 +|      html = render(view)
     1493 +|      # Zero-JS <details> section with the 5 entry headers.
     1494 +|      assert html =~ "Thought process"
     1495 +|      assert html =~ "(5)"
     1496 +|      # System/user entry contents.
     1497 +|      assert html =~ "you are genesis"
     1498 +|      assert html =~ "New message: hi"
     1499 +|      # Assistant entry: reasoning details render from reasoning_details; the
     1500 +|      # :thinking content part's text is EXCLUDED from the entry content (it
     1501 +|      # would otherwise duplicate the reasoning text).
     1502 +|      refute html =~ "let me inspect the source"
     1503 +|      assert html =~ "think"
     1504 +|      # Tool entry: tool-name header + result content.
     1505 +|      assert html =~ "Tool Result: spawn_investigator"
     1506 +|      assert html =~ "result..."
     1507 +|      # Tool-call row via the agents-page ToolCallDisplay contract.
     1508 +|      assert html =~ "spawn_investigator"
     1509 +|    end
     1510 +|  end
     1511 +|
     1512 +|  describe "assistant raw toggle & copy flash" do
     1513 +|    test "raw toggle flips exactly one message between markdown and raw view", %{conn: conn} do
     1514 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1515 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_fetch_seq]
     1516 +|
     1517 +|      # Two FINALIZED assistant entries (both render .md-content) plus a
     1518 +|      # streaming tail. The history fetch materializes the seeded transcript
     1519 +|      # into the rendered html via the real assign path (replace_state alone
     1520 +|      # never pushes a diff — same pattern as the badge tests).
     1521 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
     1522 +|        chat_status: :running,
     1523 +|        chat_task_id: "t1",
     1524 +|        chat_agent_id: 1001,
     1525 +|        transcript: [
     1526 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
     1527 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "**first** md", streaming: false},
     1528 +|          %{id: "3", role: :assistant, text: "**second** md", streaming: false},
     1529 +|          %{id: "4", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     1530 +|        ]
     1531 +|      })
     1532 +|
     1533 +|      send(
     1534 +|        view.pid,
     1535 +|        {:chat_history_loaded, node(), seq, 1001,
     1536 +|         [%{role: :assistant, content: [%{text: "streamed tail"}]}]}
     1537 +|      )
     1538 +|
     1539 +|      html = render(view)
     1540 +|
     1541 +|      # NOTE: Floki.find must run on a PARSED document, never on the raw html
     1542 +|      # binary (LiveView envelope markup confuses the direct-binary path and
     1543 +|      # tag selectors come back empty) — parse first, everywhere below.
     1544 +|      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
     1545 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ".md-content")) == 2
     1546 +|
     1547 +|      # Toggle entry "2" to raw: its .md-content disappears (plain escaped
     1548 +|      # source text now), entry "3" is untouched — scope per wrapper id.
     1549 +|      html = render_click(view, "toggle_assistant_raw", %{"id" => "2"})
     1550 +|      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
     1551 +|      assert Floki.find(doc, ~s(#chat-entry-2 .md-content)) == []
     1552 +|      assert Floki.find(doc, ~s(#chat-entry-2)) |> Floki.text() =~ "**first** md"
     1553 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ~s(#chat-entry-3 .md-content))) == 1
     1554 +|      assert MapSet.member?(assigns(view).raw_entry_ids, "2")
     1555 +|      refute MapSet.member?(assigns(view).raw_entry_ids, "3")
     1556 +|
     1557 +|      # Toggle back: markdown returns for entry "2", the raw set empties.
     1558 +|      html = render_click(view, "toggle_assistant_raw", %{"id" => "2"})
     1559 +|      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
     1560 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ".md-content")) == 2
     1561 +|      refute MapSet.member?(assigns(view).raw_entry_ids, "2")
     1562 +|    end
     1563 +|
     1564 +|    test "copied event flashes the info confirmation", %{conn: conn} do
     1565 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1566 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_task_fetch_seq]
     1567 +|      seed_running_chat(view)
     1568 +|
     1569 +|      # Finalize a completed entry (the copy button exists on finalized
     1570 +|      # entries only; the hook is client-side, the event just flashes).
     1571 +|      send(
     1572 +|        view.pid,
     1573 +|        {:chat_task_loaded, node(), seq, "t1",
     1574 +|         %{status: :completed, result: {:ok, %{result: "final answer"}}}}
     1575 +|      )
     1576 +|
     1577 +|      render(view)
     1578 +|
     1579 +|      html = render_hook(view, "copied", %{})

     1584 +|
     1585 +|  describe "chat persistence / restore (ChatHistory)" do
     1586 +|    test "transcript and status persist across a remount", %{conn: conn} do
     1587 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1588 +|      chat_id = assigns(view).chat_id
     1589 +|      assert chat_id != nil
     1590 +|
     1591 +|      # Fully SYNTHETIC running chat: seed the state a real send would produce
     1592 +|      # (the user entry "persist me please" + an empty streaming assistant
     1593 +|      # entry, refs set) via seed_chat_state — the real reflect runtime must
     1594 +|      # never run in this suite (see the setup's fail-fast block).
     1595 +|      # seed_chat_state bypasses __changed__ tracking, so
     1596 +|      # the seeded transcript is materialized into rendered html by the same
     1597 +|      # real assign path the badge/persistence tests use (a matching task
     1598 +|      # event), then finalized with the exact {:task_updated, ...} shape the
     1599 +|      # core broadcasts.
     1600 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
     1601 +|        chat_status: :running,
     1602 +|        chat_task_id: "t_persist",
     1603 +|        chat_agent_id: 1001,
     1604 +|        chat_node: node(),
     1605 +|        transcript: [
     1606 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "persist me please", streaming: false},
     1607 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     1608 +|        ]
     1609 +|      })
     1610 +|
     1611 +|      # Materialize the seeded state (a matching non-terminal task event runs
     1612 +|      # handle_task_event's assign/persist path) and persist the running state
     1613 +|      # into ChatHistory through the REAL persist point.
     1614 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t_persist", :running, node()})
     1615 +|      render(view)
     1616 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :running
     1617 +|      assert EvoDash.ChatHistory.get_state(chat_id).chat_status == :running
     1618 +|
     1619 +|      # Deterministic terminal event (the same event the finalize_failed
     1620 +|      # helper drives): :failed finalizes the transcript and clears the refs.
     1621 +|      finalize_failed(view, "t_persist")
     1622 +|
     1623 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     1624 +|      # The terminal event persisted the full state into ChatHistory.
     1625 +|      stored = EvoDash.ChatHistory.get_state(chat_id)
     1626 +|      assert stored != nil
     1627 +|      assert Enum.any?(stored.transcript, &(&1.role == :user and &1.text == "persist me please"))
     1628 +|      # "Close the page" (stop the view process) and remount: attach_chat
     1629 +|      # restores the CURRENT chat — transcript and status.
     1630 +|      GenServer.stop(view.pid)
     1631 +|      {:ok, view2, html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1632 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_id == chat_id
     1633 +|      assert html =~ "persist me please"
     1634 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_status == :idle
     1635 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_id == nil
     1636 +|    end
     1637 +|
     1638 +|    test "reconciliation finalizes from a terminal row that carries an archive (lite read)", %{
     1639 +|      conn: conn
     1640 +|    } do
     1641 +|      # The result still carries the legacy `archive_records` payload (older
     1642 +|      # rows); the per-agent archive itself is no longer a task-row column. The
     1643 +|      # terminal path needs only status/result/error, so the lite read must
     1644 +|      # still finalize the transcript.
     1645 +|      insert_task_fixture!(
     1646 +|        id: "t_archived",
     1647 +|        type: :reflect,
     1648 +|        status: :completed,
     1649 +|        opts: [mode: "reflect", objective: "New message: archived"],
     1650 +|        project_path: nil,
     1651 +|        result:
     1652 +|          {:ok,
     1653 +|           %{
     1654 +|             result: "final answer with archive",
     1655 +|             commit_sha: nil,
     1656 +|             branch_name: nil,
     1657 +|             tag: nil,
     1658 +|             archive_records: [%{"agent_id" => 1, "objective" => "probe"}]
     1659 +|           }}
     1660 +|      )
     1661 +|
     1662 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1663 +|      chat_id = assigns(view).chat_id
     1664 +|
     1665 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
     1666 +|        chat_status: :running,
     1667 +|        chat_task_id: "t_archived",
     1668 +|        chat_agent_id: 1001,
     1669 +|        chat_node: node(),
     1670 +|        transcript: [
     1671 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
     1672 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     1673 +|        ]
     1674 +|      })
     1675 +|
     1676 +|      # Persist the mid-run state via a REAL persist point (task event).
     1677 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t_archived", :running, node()})
     1678 +|      render(view)
     1679 +|      # Leave the page and remount: the ONE-SHOT reconciliation fetch lands
     1680 +|      # the terminal row and finalizes the transcript from `result`.
     1681 +|      GenServer.stop(view.pid)
     1682 +|      {:ok, view2, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1683 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_id == chat_id
     1684 +|
     1685 +|      wait_until(fn -> assigns(view2).chat_status == :idle end)
     1686 +|      html = render(view2)
     1687 +|      assert html =~ "final answer with archive"
     1688 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_status == :completed
     1689 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_id == nil
     1690 +|    end
     1691 +|
     1692 +|    test "mid-run remount reconciles the task ONCE and applies the terminal state", %{
     1693 +|      conn: conn
     1694 +|    } do
     1695 +|      # The task "completes while the page is closed": the store row is
     1696 +|      # ALREADY terminal when the view remounts.
     1697 +|      insert_task_fixture!(
     1698 +|        id: "t_away",
     1699 +|        type: :reflect,
     1700 +|        status: :completed,
     1701 +|        opts: [mode: "reflect", objective: "New message: away"],
     1702 +|        project_path: nil,
     1703 +|        result:
     1704 +|          {:ok, %{result: "finished while away", commit_sha: nil, branch_name: nil, tag: nil}}
     1705 +|      )
     1706 +|
     1707 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1708 +|      chat_id = assigns(view).chat_id
     1709 +|
     1710 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
     1711 +|        chat_status: :running,
     1712 +|        chat_task_id: "t_away",
     1713 +|        chat_agent_id: 1001,
     1714 +|        chat_node: node(),
     1715 +|        transcript: [
     1716 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
     1717 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     1718 +|        ]
     1719 +|      })
     1720 +|
     1721 +|      # Persist the mid-run state via a REAL persist point (task event).
     1722 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t_away", :running, node()})
     1723 +|      render(view)
     1724 +|      assert EvoDash.ChatHistory.get_state(chat_id).chat_status == :running
     1725 +|      # Leave the page and remount.
     1726 +|      GenServer.stop(view.pid)
     1727 +|      {:ok, view2, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1728 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_id == chat_id
     1729 +|      # attach_chat restored the running chat; reconcile_chat spawned the
     1730 +|      # ONE-SHOT task fetch (chat_task_fetch_seq 0→1) which lands the
     1731 +|      # terminal :completed row → finalize + clear refs.
     1732 +|      wait_until(fn -> assigns(view2).chat_status == :idle end)
     1733 +|      html = render(view2)
     1734 +|      assert html =~ "finished while away"
     1735 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_id == nil
     1736 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_agent_id == nil
     1737 +|      # The final badge stays (clear_task_refs keeps :chat_task_status).
     1738 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_status == :completed
     1739 +|      assert html =~ "Completed"
     1740 +|    end
     1741 +|
     1742 +|    test "mid-run remount keeps the refs when the task is still alive", %{conn: conn} do
     1743 +|      insert_task_fixture!(
     1744 +|        id: "t_alive",
     1745 +|        type: :reflect,
     1746 +|        status: :running,
     1747 +|        opts: [mode: "reflect", objective: "New message: alive"],
     1748 +|        project_path: nil
     1749 +|      )
     1750 +|
     1751 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1752 +|      chat_id = assigns(view).chat_id

     1755 +|        chat_status: :running,

     1757 +|        chat_agent_id: 1001,
     1758 +|        chat_node: node(),
     1759 +|        transcript: [
     1760 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
     1761 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     1762 +|        ]
     1763 +|      })
     1764 +|

     1766 +|      render(view)
     1767 +|      GenServer.stop(view.pid)
     1768 +|      {:ok, view2, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     1769 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_id == chat_id
     1770 +|      # The one-shot reconcile fetch finds a STILL-RUNNING task: badge/status
     1771 +|      # refreshed, refs KEPT (further task/agent events must still match).
     1772 +|      wait_until(fn -> assigns(view2)[:chat_task_fetch_seq] == 1 end)
     1773 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_id == "t_alive"
     1774 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_status == :running
     1775 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_agent_id == 1001
     1776 +|      assert assigns(view2).chat_task_status == :running
     1777 +|    end

     2140 +|  describe "production-mimicking crash repro" do
     2141 +|    test "real reflect task + real agent ETS rows + real broadcasts end-to-end", %{conn: conn} do
     2142 +|      # A REAL agent id in the scheduler's id space (integer, like production).
     2143 +|      agent_id = 400_000 + rem(System.unique_integer([:positive]), 50_000)
     2144 +|      # REAL scheduler ETS rows: SchedMeta + AgentState with a REAL
     2145 +|      # %ReqLLM.Context{} whose history includes thinking parts, tool_calls,
     2146 +|      # reasoning_details, nil metadata AND an assistant message with
     2147 +|      # content: [] (the empty-list hardening case).
     2148 +|      spec = %EvoGit.AgentSpec{
     2149 +|        context_node: %EvoGit.Core.ContextNode{path: "/tmp/x", repo: "/tmp/x"},
     2150 +|        phylo_node: %EvoGit.Core.PhyloGraphNode{
     2151 +|          repo: "/tmp/x",
     2152 +|          base_commit: "abc",

     2154 +|        },
     2155 +|        agent_module: EvoGit.Agents.SelfReflective,

     2157 +|      }
     2158 +|
     2159 +|      meta = %EvoGit.AgentScheduler.SchedMeta{
     2160 +|        id: agent_id,
     2161 +|        depth: 0,

     2164 +|      }

     2166 +|      :ets.insert(:evogit_sched_meta, {agent_id, meta})

     2195 +|        ]
     2196 +|      })

     2199 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, "t_crash", :running, node()})
     2200 +|      html = render(view)

     2230 +|        id: "t_crash",
