     2254 +|  describe "sidebar robustness" do
     2255 +|    test "warm ActiveTasks hub seeds the sidebar instantly on mount (no broadcast needed)", %{
     2256 +|      conn: conn
     2257 +|    } do
     2258 +|      # Pre-warm the shared hub for the local context with a reflect-style
     2259 +|      # :running summary map (the shape an applied fetch result writes — the
     2260 +|      # 16-key EvoGit.TaskRegistry.list_tasks_summary/1 projection). The hub
     2261 +|      # seed is SYNCHRONOUS (node_aware.ex on_mount), so the VERY FIRST render
     2262 +|      # already carries the sidebar — no task_updated broadcast, no debounce
     2263 +|      # sleep: the exact no-blink contract. (The connected-mount
     2264 +|      # staleness-catch-up fetch still fires afterwards and is stale-guarded;
     2265 +|      # it lands after this html assertion and does not affect the first
     2266 +|      # paint.)
     2267 +|      EvoDash.ActiveTasks.put(
     2268 +|        nil,
     2269 +|        node(),
     2270 +|        [
     2271 +|          %{
     2272 +|            id: "reflect_instant",
     2273 +|            type: :reflect,
     2274 +|            status: :running,
     2275 +|            review_status: nil,
     2276 +|            started_at: DateTime.utc_now(),
     2277 +|            finished_at: nil,
     2278 +|            project_path: nil,
     2279 +|            opts: [mode: "reflect", objective: "New message: instant"],
     2280 +|            branch_name: nil,
     2281 +|            model_id: nil,
     2282 +|            agent_count: 0,
     2283 +|            base_sha: nil,
     2284 +|            commit_sha: nil,
     2285 +|            lease_expires_at: nil,
     2286 +|            updated_at: nil,
     2287 +|            error: nil
     2288 +|          }
     2289 +|        ],
     2290 +|        []
     2291 +|      )
     2292 +|
     2293 +|      # Both the dead render and the connected mount seed :running_tasks /
     2294 +|      # :pending_tasks synchronously from the hub, so the mount html already
     2295 +|      # contains the running reflect task's sidebar row.
     2296 +|      {:ok, _view, html} = live(conn, "/help")
     2297 +|
     2298 +|      assert html =~ "Active Tasks"
     2299 +|      assert html =~ "New message: instant"
     2300 +|    end

     2384 +|  describe "stop / cancel flow" do
     2385 +|    test "stop cancels a pending task and finalizes it", %{conn: conn} do
     2386 +|      fixture_id = insert_task_fixture!(status: :pending)
     2387 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     2388 +|
     2389 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{
     2390 +|        chat_status: :running,
     2391 +|        chat_task_id: fixture_id,
     2392 +|        transcript: [
     2393 +|          %{id: "1", role: :user, text: "hello", streaming: false},
     2394 +|          %{id: "2", role: :assistant, text: "", streaming: true}
     2395 +|        ]
     2396 +|      })
     2397 +|
     2398 +|      # Materialize a diff so the rendered html reflects the running state: a
     2399 +|      # matching :running task event triggers async_lookup_agent → assign. The
     2400 +|      # Stop-button state itself is NOT diffed here (chat_status was injected
     2401 +|      # via replace_state, bypassing assign/2's __changed__ tracking), so the
     2402 +|      # html-level "enabled" check is unreliable — assert the accurate assigns
     2403 +|      # value instead.
     2404 +|      send(view.pid, {:task_updated, fixture_id, :running, node()})
     2405 +|      render(view)
     2406 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :running
     2407 +|      # render_click("stop") runs the REAL cancel: :pending → immediate
     2408 +|      # :cancelled + broadcast. The view shows :cancelling (the async task
     2409 +|      # fetch may already have landed → :idle; accept both).
     2410 +|      render_click(view, "stop", %{})
     2411 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status in [:cancelling, :idle]
     2412 +|      # Deterministic finalize: inject the fetched :cancelled task with the
     2413 +|      # CURRENT seq. If the real async fetch already landed (chat_task_id nil),
     2414 +|      # the injected message is stale-dropped — but the finalize already
     2415 +|      # happened, so the assertions still hold. Both paths are idempotent.
     2416 +|      seq = assigns(view)[:chat_task_fetch_seq]
     2417 +|
     2418 +|      send(
     2419 +|        view.pid,
     2420 +|        {:chat_task_loaded, node(), seq, fixture_id, %{status: :cancelled, result: nil}}
     2421 +|      )
     2422 +|
     2423 +|      html = render(view)
     2424 +|      assert html =~ "Stopped."
     2425 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     2426 +|      assert disabled?(html, ~s(button[phx-click="stop"]))
     2427 +|    end
     2428 +|
     2429 +|    test "stop with no task is a no-op", %{conn: conn} do
     2430 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     2431 +|      html = render_click(view, "stop", %{})
     2432 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_status == :idle
     2433 +|      assert assigns(view).chat_task_id == nil
     2434 +|      assert html =~ "Start a conversation"
     2435 +|    end
     2436 +|  end

     2438 +|  # Command-approval request fixtures (security levels 2 & 3 of the core's
     2439 +|  # run_command tool). Defaults match the seeded running chat used by the

     2441 +|  # task-id path of own_request?/2 does the matching.

     2455 +|  end

     2488 +|    )

     2491 +|  defp approval_button(id, decision) do
     2492 +|    ~s(button[phx-click="approval_response"][phx-value-request_id="#{id}"][phx-value-decision="#{decision}"
])

     2495 +|  defp install_blocking_approval_responder do
     2496 +|    test_pid = self()
     2497 +|
     2498 +|    Application.put_env(:evo_dash, :approval_responder, fn source, id, decision ->
     2499 +|      send(test_pid, {:approval_response_called, self(), source, id, decision})
     2500 +|
     2501 +|      receive do
     2502 +|        {:respond, result} -> result
     2503 +|      after
     2504 +|        5000 -> exit(:approval_test_timeout)
     2505 +|      end
     2506 +|    end)
     2507 +|  end

     2541 +|  describe "command approvals (security levels 2 & 3)" do
     2542 +|    setup do
     2543 +|      keys = [:approval_responder, :approval_pending_runner]
     2544 +|      originals = Map.new(keys, &{&1, Application.fetch_env(:evo_dash, &1)})
     2545 +|
     2546 +|      # A restored chat must never query an ambient singleton's pending list.
     2547 +|      # Recovery tests below override this list-returning runner explicitly.
     2548 +|      Application.put_env(:evo_dash, :approval_pending_runner, fn _source -> [] end)
     2549 +|
     2550 +|      on_exit(fn ->
     2551 +|        Enum.each(originals, fn
     2552 +|          {key, {:ok, value}} -> Application.put_env(:evo_dash, key, value)
     2553 +|          {key, :error} -> Application.delete_env(:evo_dash, key)
     2554 +|        end)
     2555 +|      end)
     2556 +|
     2557 +|      :ok
     2558 +|    end
     2559 +|

     2571 +|      assert html =~ "badge badge-sm badge-info"
     2572 +|      # The command shows in a <code> block; args as plain escaped text below it.
     2573 +|      assert html =~ "NavigateToPage.navigate"
     2574 +|      assert html =~ "guide the user to the Settings page"
     2575 +|
     2576 +|      # Both buttons: Confirm (approve) + Deny (deny), each carrying the
     2577 +|      # phx-click + request id.
     2578 +|      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)
     2579 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ~s([phx-click="approval_response"]))) == 2
     2580 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ~s([phx-value-decision="approve"]))) == 1
     2581 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ~s([phx-value-decision="deny"]))) == 1
     2582 +|      assert length(Floki.find(doc, ~s([phx-value-request_id="req-1"]))) == 2
     2583 +|
     2584 +|      assert [entry] = assigns(view)[:pending_approvals]
     2585 +|      assert entry.request_id == "req-1"

     2587 +|

     2604 +|    test "requests for other tasks, agents, and nodes are ignored", %{conn: conn} do

     2626 +|
     2627 +|    test "approval_resolved removes the card (unknown ids are no-ops)", %{conn: conn} do

     2646 +|    test "confirm/deny retain a busy card until the asynchronous responder succeeds", %{

     2649 +|      install_blocking_approval_responder()

     2652 +|

     2654 +|        send(view.pid, {:approval_requested, l2_request(request_id: id)})
     2655 +|        render(view)
     2656 +|

     2658 +|          view

     2663 +|        assert source == node()

     2665 +|        assert [%{request_id: ^id}] = assigns(view)[:pending_approvals]
     2666 +|        assert disabled?(html, approval_button(id, "approve"))
     2667 +|        assert disabled?(html, approval_button(id, "deny"))

     2673 +|        assert assigns(view).approval_responses[id] == seq
     2674 +|        refute_receive {:approval_response_called, _, _, _, _}, 50

     2677 +|        wait_until(fn -> assigns(view)[:pending_approvals] == [] end)

     2679 +|        assert assigns(view).approval_responses == %{}
     2680 +|        assert assigns(view).approval_error == nil
     2681 +|        refute present?(html, approval_button(id, decision))
     2682 +|      end

     2730 +|      assert assigns(view).approval_error == nil

     2736 +|      refute html =~ "The assistant wants your attention"
     2737 +|    end

     2814 +|               sudo.code

     2817 +|      refute html =~ "Allow this Elixir code once?"

     2819 +|      assert length(assigns(view).pending_approvals) == 1

     2822 +|    test "evaluator ownership and resolutions use the source node, never the target runtime", %{
     2823 +|      conn: conn
     2824 +|    } do
     2825 +|      install_blocking_approval_responder()
     2826 +|      {:ok, view, _html} = live(conn, "/help")
     2827 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{chat_status: :running, chat_task_id: "t1", chat_agent_id: 1001})
     2828 +|
     2829 +|      # Neither a matching target nor a matching task can authenticate another
     2830 +|      # source node; evaluator requests without a source stamp are rejected too.
     2831 +|      for request <- [
     2832 +|            eval_request(node: :foreign@source, target_node: node()),
     2833 +|            eval_request(node: nil),
     2834 +|            eval_request(task_id: "other-task", agent_id: nil)
     2835 +|          ] do
     2836 +|        send(view.pid, {:approval_requested, request})
     2837 +|      end
     2838 +|
     2839 +|      render(view)
     2840 +|      assert assigns(view).pending_approvals == []
     2841 +|
     2842 +|      # String/integer agent refs match even without the task ref. The target
     2843 +|      # is deliberately remote; the response must still go to LOCAL source.
     2844 +|      request = eval_request(task_id: nil, agent_id: "1001")
     2845 +|      send(view.pid, {:approval_requested, request})
     2846 +|      html = render(view)
     2847 +|      assert html =~ "Target runtime: target@runtime"
     2848 +|      assert [%{request_id: "eval-1"}] = assigns(view).pending_approvals
     2849 +|
     2850 +|      send(view.pid, {:approval_resolved, "eval-1", :approve, :foreign@source})
     2851 +|      render(view)
     2852 +|      assert [%{request_id: "eval-1"}] = assigns(view).pending_approvals
     2853 +|
     2854 +|      view |> element(approval_button("eval-1", "approve")) |> render_click()
     2855 +|      assert_receive {:approval_response_called, worker, source, "eval-1", "approve"}, 2000
     2856 +|      assert source == node()
     2857 +|      refute source == request.target_node
     2858 +|
     2859 +|      send(view.pid, {:approval_resolved, "eval-1", :approve, node()})
     2860 +|      html = render(view)
     2861 +|      assert assigns(view).pending_approvals == []
     2862 +|      assert assigns(view).approval_responses == %{}
     2863 +|      refute present?(html, approval_button("eval-1", "approve"))
     2864 +|
     2865 +|      # A late error from the now-resolved response cannot resurrect its card
     2866 +|      # or show an error. Monitor the worker before draining the page mailbox.
     2867 +|      monitor = Process.monitor(worker)
     2868 +|      send(worker, {:respond, {:error, :nodedown}})
     2869 +|      assert_receive {:DOWN, ^monitor, :process, ^worker, :normal}, 2000
     2870 +|      render(view)
     2871 +|      assert assigns(view).approval_error == nil
     2872 +|      assert assigns(view).pending_approvals == []
     2873 +|    end

     2947 +|    test "an in-flight recovery keeps live requests and cannot resurrect a resolved card", %{
     2948 +|      conn: conn

     2961 +|      end)

     2996 +|               ["newer", "req-1"]

     3003 +|    test "a recovery result captured for the previous chat scope is discarded", %{conn: conn} do
     3004 +|      seed_restorable_approval_chat()

     3006 +|

     3015 +|      end)
     3016 +|

     3026 +|      seed_chat_state(view, %{chat_id: "different-chat"})

     3032 +|      assert assigns(view).approval_error == nil
     3033 +|

     3035 +|      monitor = Process.monitor(worker)

     3037 +|      assert_receive {:DOWN, ^monitor, :process, ^worker, :normal}, 2000

     3056 +|  describe "genesis source gate" do
     3057 +|    # The gate's availability/clone runners are read from the app env AT SPAWN
     3058 +|    # TIME inside EvoDash.TaskSupervisor children, so tests stub them BEFORE
     3059 +|    # live/3 and restore the originals in on_exit (the system_live_test.exs
     3060 +|    # "genesis source card" restore_env_value/2 idiom). The default runners
     3061 +|    # degrade to {:unavailable, :function_missing} while the core
     3062 +|    # EvoGit.SelfReflectiveSource backend is absent, so the gate stays OFF for
     3063 +|    # every test that does not stub it.
     3064 +|    setup do
     3065 +|      keys = [:source_availability_runner, :source_clone_runner]
     3066 +|      originals = Map.new(keys, fn key -> {key, Application.get_env(:evo_dash, key)} end)
     3067 +|
     3068 +|      on_exit(fn ->
     3069 +|        Enum.each(originals, fn {key, original} -> restore_env_value(key, original) end)
     3070 +|      end)
     3071 +|
     3072 +|      :ok
     3073 +|    end
     3074 +|
     3075 +|    test "known-unavailable source shows the gate and blocks the composer", %{conn: conn} do

     3139 +|    test "a successful download clears the gate (busy → post-clone re-check)", %{conn: conn} do

     3144 +|      assert present?(html, "#genesis-source-gate")

     3147 +|      # "Cloning…" busy state asserted below is assigned SYNCHRONOUSLY by the

     3149 +|      # the render taken immediately after that handler returns (the LiveView
     3150 +|      # process cannot process the clone-result message before then) — so the
     3151 +|      # busy markup is deterministic and the stub needs no artificial delay.

     3265 +|      assert EvoDash.ChatHistory.current_chat_id() == new_id

     3317 +|    test "source gate never renders on a remote node (local-only)", %{conn: conn} do
     3318 +|      test_pid = self()
     3319 +|      original = Application.get_env(:evo_dash, :source_availability_runner)
     3320 +|      on_exit(fn -> restore_env_value(:source_availability_runner, original) end)
     3321 +|
     3322 +|      # A loud runner that would flag any invocation — the local-only gate must
     3323 +|      # NEVER spawn it while viewing a remote node.
     3324 +|      Application.put_env(:evo_dash, :source_availability_runner, fn ->
     3325 +|        send(test_pid, :availability_runner_called)
     3326 +|        false
     3327 +|      end)

     3330 +|      html = render(view)
     3331 +|
     3332 +|      assert assigns(view).current_node == :"genesis_remote@127.0.0.1"
     3333 +|      # No check is spawned for a remote node → the runner is never called and
     3334 +|      # source_available stays "unknown".
     3335 +|      #
     3336 +|      # The 50ms window (down from 200ms) is sufficient because the failure mode
     3337 +|      # is impossible BY CONSTRUCTION and, when it could occur, is immediate:
     3338 +|      # `maybe_check_source/1` is the ONLY caller of the spawn helper and gates
     3339 +|      # it on `SourceGate.visible?(current_node)` (node in [nil, node()]), and
     3340 +|      # this test views a remote node — so a regression would call the runner
     3341 +|      # during the CONNECTED MOUNT (handle_params), i.e. before/while `live/3`
     3342 +|      # returns. The runner then sends its flag synchronously on the very next
     3343 +|      # TaskSupervisor scheduler slot, far inside a 50ms budget. The
     3344 +|      # `assert assigns(view).source_available == nil` line below is the
     3345 +|      # durable assertion on the same failure mode (a spawned check with this
     3346 +|      # node would make the gate known-unavailable → false).
     3347 +|      refute_receive :availability_runner_called, 50
     3348 +|      assert assigns(view).source_available == nil
     3349 +|
     3350 +|      # The source gate is local-only, but a missing remote model snapshot
     3351 +|      # independently disables sending until a valid node-scoped load succeeds.
     3352 +|      refute present?(html, "#genesis-source-gate")
     3353 +|      refute html =~ "Download the Genesis source to start chatting."
     3354 +|      refute disabled?(html, ~s(textarea[name="message"]))
     3355 +|      assert disabled?(html, ~s(#chat-form button[type="submit"]))
     3356 +|      assert html =~ "Show my running tasks"
     3357 +|    end
     3358 +|  end
     3359 +|end
     3360 +|
     3361 +|# A minimal GenServer that stands in for a real connection manager in
     3362 +|# `EvoGit.RemoteConnection.Registry`, so `EvoGit.RemoteConnection.status/1`
     3363 +|# resolves a configured status for a target id without starting any SSH
     3364 +|# machinery (same pattern as `EvoDashWeb.NodeAwareTest.ConnectionManager`).
     3365 +|# The process dies (and its Registry entry is auto-removed) at test end via
     3366 +|# `start_supervised!`.
     3367 +|defmodule EvoDashWeb.HomeLiveTest.ConnectionManager do
     3368 +|  use GenServer
     3369 +|
     3370 +|  def start_link(args) do
     3371 +|    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, args)
     3372 +|  end
     3373 +|
     3374 +|  @impl true
     3375 +|  def init({target_id, status}) do
     3376 +|    Registry.register(EvoGit.RemoteConnection.Registry, target_id, :status)
     3377 +|    {:ok, status}
     3378 +|  end
     3379 +|
     3380 +|  @impl true
     3381 +|  def handle_call(:status, _from, status), do: {:reply, status, status}
     3382 +|
     3383 +|  describe "assistant entry routes" do
     3384 +|    test "GET / renders the assistant page", %{conn: conn} do
     3385 +|      {:ok, _view, html} = live(conn, "/")
     3386 +|
     3387 +|      assert html =~ "Assistant"
     3388 +|      assert html =~ "chat-form"
     3389 +|      assert html =~ "assistant-workspace"
     3390 +|    end
     3391 +|
     3392 +|    test "GET /help still renders the assistant page", %{conn: conn} do
     3393 +|      {:ok, _view, html} = live(conn, "/help")
     3394 +|
     3395 +|      assert html =~ "chat-form"
     3396 +|    end
     3397 +|  end
     3398 +|end
     3399 +|

 317      -|    # On the dead render (initial HTTP request), redirect first-time users to

      315 +|  @impl true
      316 +|  def mount(_params, _session, socket) do
      317 +|    # On the dead render (initial HTTP request), redirect first-time users to
      318 +|    # /welcome (same pattern as ProjectsLive). Runs ONLY on the dead render;
      319 +|    # connected mounts skip it to avoid redirect loops.
      320 +|    onboarding_needed =
      321 +|      !connected?(socket) and
      322 +|        if Code.ensure_loaded?(EvoGit.Config.VersionState) do
      323 +|          EvoGit.Config.VersionState.onboarding_needed?()
      324 +|        else
      325 +|          false
      326 +|        end
      327 +|
      328 +|    if onboarding_needed do
      329 +|      {:ok, push_navigate(socket, to: "/welcome")}
      330 +|    else
      331 +|      if connected?(socket) do
      332 +|        Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(EvoGit.PubSub, "tasks")
      333 +|        Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(EvoGit.PubSub, "agents")
      334 +|        # Evaluator and legacy command approvals both use this topic. Recovery
      335 +|        # starts only after this subscription, chat restoration and node resolution.
      336 +|        Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(EvoGit.PubSub, "approvals")
      337 +|      end
      338 +|
      339 +|      socket =
      340 +|        socket
      341 +|        |> assign(base_assigns())
      342 +|        |> ApprovalWorkflow.init()
      343 +|        |> attach_chat()
      344 +|
      345 +|      {:ok, socket}
      346 +|    end
      347 +|  end
      348 +|
      349 +|  # Seed assigns for every mount (fresh state; `attach_chat/1` then overlays
      350 +|  # the persisted chat on connected mounts). Includes the live-chat keys added
      351 +|  # by the ChatHistory persistence + the assistant task-card (`chat_id`,
      352 +|  # `chat_node`, `chat_task_status`, `thought_process`).
      353 +|  defp base_assigns do
      354 +|    %{
      355 +|      transcript: Transcript.new(),
      356 +|      chat_draft: "",
      357 +|      chat_status: :idle,
      358 +|      chat_task_id: nil,
      359 +|      chat_agent_id: nil,
      360 +|      agent_message_count: nil,
      361 +|      chat_task_status: nil,
      362 +|      thought_process: [],
      363 +|      chat_node: nil,
      364 +|      chat_id: nil,
      365 +|      chat_fetch_seq: 0,
      366 +|      chat_task_fetch_seq: 0,
      367 +|      shift_down: false,
      368 +|      selected_model_id: nil,
      369 +|      # Ephemeral UI state: transcript entry ids currently shown in RAW (plain
      370 +|      # text) view instead of the markdown render. Never persisted — entries
      371 +|      # are cleared with the transcript on reset_chat.
      372 +|      raw_entry_ids: MapSet.new(),
      373 +|      # The last approval scope reconciled on this connection. Recovery starts
      374 +|      # after handle_params resolves the node, never against mount's local seed.
      375 +|      # ApprovalWorkflow owns all ephemeral cards, response tokens and errors.
      376 +|      approval_context: nil,
      377 +|      provider_profiles: [],
      378 +|      model_selections: [],
      379 +|      model_spec: nil,
      380 +|      model_selection_scope: nil,
      381 +|      model_selection_error: nil,
      382 +|      model_selection_restore: nil,
      383 +|      model_selection_resolved: false,
      384 +|      model_selection_enabled: false,
      385 +|      # Config snapshots/bundles are server-only and never enter ChatState.
      386 +|      model_snapshot: nil,
      387 +|      model_selection_bundle: nil,
      388 +|      model_select_loading: false,
      389 +|      model_select_load_error: nil,
      390 +|      # Monotonic stale-guard for the ASYNC remote model-profile load (a remote
      391 +|      # node resolves its profiles via an `:erpc` config fetch, so the load is
      392 +|      # NEVER run in the LiveView process — see assign_model_select/1). Monotonic
      393 +|      # and never reset (a reset could make an older in-flight result apply).
      394 +|      model_select_seq: 0,
      395 +|      # Genesis-source gate (EPHEMERAL — never persisted to ChatState): the
      396 +|      # /help chat needs the managed Genesis source checkout, which is usually
      397 +|      # absent on a fresh install. `source_available` = the latest availability
      398 +|      # result (`nil` unknown | `true` | `false` | `{:unavailable, reason}`) —
      399 +|      # only a KNOWN `false` blocks sending; `source_check_loading` mirrors the
      400 +|      # in-flight check window; `source_check_seq` is the monotonic stale-guard
      401 +|      # counter (SEPARATE from chat_fetch_seq/chat_task_fetch_seq); `source_busy`
      402 +|      # is a truthy marker while a download runs. Local-node only.
      403 +|      source_available: nil,
      404 +|      source_check_loading: false,
      405 +|      source_check_seq: 0,
      406 +|      source_busy: nil,
      407 +|      current_path: ~p"/help",
      408 +|      # Assistant workspace gate: chatting requires an explicitly chosen
      409 +|      # workspace. `recent_projects` is loaded node-aware OFF the LiveView
      410 +|      # process (see assign_recent_projects/1); `workspace` is the chosen
      411 +|      # absolute path (persisted per chat via ChatState) or nil.
      412 +|      workspace: nil,
      413 +|      recent_projects: [],
      414 +|      recent_projects_seq: 0
      415 +|    }
      416 +|  end
      417 +|
      418 +|  # Only the resolved node may supply a snapshot. Pending targets must never
      419 +|  # read local config; every load invalidates earlier async results, including
      420 +|  # a switch between two pending targets that share the local node fallback.
      421 +|  defp assign_model_select(socket) do
      422 +|    {_target_id, target_node} = selection_scope(socket)
      423 +|    seq = socket.assigns.model_select_seq + 1
      424 +|
      425 +|    socket =
      426 +|      assign(socket,
      427 +|        model_select_seq: seq,
      428 +|        model_snapshot: nil,
      429 +|        model_selection_bundle: nil,
      430 +|        provider_profiles: [],
      431 +|        model_selections: [],
      432 +|        model_selection_enabled: false,
      433 +|        model_select_loading: false,
      434 +|        model_select_load_error: nil
      435 +|      )
      436 +|
      437 +|    cond do
      438 +|      pending_remote?(socket) ->
      439 +|        assign(socket, model_select_load_error: :remote_not_connected)
      440 +|
      441 +|      target_node == node() ->
      442 +|        apply_model_snapshot(socket, ModelSelect.load(target_node))
      443 +|
      444 +|      connected?(socket) ->
      445 +|        case EvoDash.SafeCall.start(self(), {:model_select_loaded, seq, target_node}, fn ->
      446 +|               ModelSelect.load(target_node)
      447 +|             end) do
      448 +|          {:ok, _pid} ->
      449 +|            assign(socket, model_select_loading: true)
      450 +|
      451 +|          {:error, reason} ->
      452 +|            # No result message is sent when the supervisor cannot start the task.
      453 +|            apply_model_snapshot(socket, {:error, reason})
      454 +|        end
      455 +|
      456 +|      true ->
      457 +|        socket
      458 +|    end
      459 +|  end
      460 +|
      461 +|  defp apply_model_snapshot(socket, {:ok, %{node: target_node} = snapshot}) do
      462 +|    if target_node == elem(selection_scope(socket), 1) and not pending_remote?(socket) do
      463 +|      bundle = LLMSelection.from_config(snapshot.config, snapshot.model_selection_enabled)
      464 +|
      465 +|      socket
      466 +|      |> assign(
      467 +|        model_snapshot: snapshot,
      468 +|        model_selection_bundle: bundle,
      469 +|        provider_profiles: bundle.provider_profiles,
      470 +|        model_selections: bundle.model_selections,
      471 +|        model_selection_enabled: bundle.model_selection_enabled,
      472 +|        model_select_loading: false,
      473 +|        model_select_load_error: nil
      474 +|      )
      475 +|      |> reconcile_model_selection()
      476 +|    else
      477 +|      socket
      478 +|    end
      479 +|  end
      480 +|
      481 +|  defp apply_model_snapshot(socket, {:error, reason}) do
      482 +|    assign(socket, model_select_loading: false, model_select_load_error: reason)
      483 +|  end
      484 +|
      485 +|  defp reconcile_model_selection(socket) do
      486 +|    # Malformed persisted specs remain visibly invalid until the user chooses
      487 +|    # again. Valid-but-stale choices are reconciled by the shared helper.
      488 +|    if socket.assigns.model_selection_error do
      489 +|      socket
      490 +|    else
      491 +|      candidate =
      492 +|        if is_nil(socket.assigns.model_spec),
      493 +|          do: socket.assigns.selected_model_id,
      494 +|          else: socket.assigns.model_spec
      495 +|
      496 +|      spec = LLMSelection.reconcile(socket.assigns.model_selection_bundle, candidate)
      497 +|
      498 +|      socket
      499 +|      |> assign(model_spec: spec, selected_model_id: nil)
      500 +|      |> persist_state()
      501 +|    end
      502 +|  end
      503 +|
      504 +|  defp update_model_selection(socket, operation, value) do
      505 +|    bundle = socket.assigns.model_selection_bundle
      506 +|
      507 +|    result =
      508 +|      if is_nil(bundle) or pending_remote?(socket) do
      509 +|        {:error, :model_selection_not_loaded}
      510 +|      else
      511 +|        apply(LLMSelection, operation, [bundle, socket.assigns.model_spec, value])
      512 +|      end
      513 +|
      514 +|    case result do
      515 +|      {:ok, spec} ->
      516 +|        {:noreply,
      517 +|         socket
      518 +|         |> assign(
      519 +|           model_spec: spec,
      520 +|           selected_model_id: nil,
      521 +|           model_selection_error: nil,
      522 +|           model_selection_scope: selection_scope(socket)
      523 +|         )
      524 +|         |> persist_state()}
      525 +|
      526 +|      {:error, _reason} ->
      527 +|        {:noreply,
      528 +|         put_flash(
      529 +|           socket,
      530 +|           :error,
      531 +|           gettext("Choose a valid provider profile, model and reasoning effort.")
      532 +|         )}
      533 +|    end
      534 +|  end
      535 +|
      536 +|  defp selection_scope(socket) do
      537 +|    {socket.assigns[:current_node_id], socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()}
      538 +|  end
      539 +|
      540 +|  defp pending_remote?(socket) do
      541 +|    {target_id, target_node} = selection_scope(socket)
      542 +|    not is_nil(target_id) and target_node == node()
      543 +|  end
      544 +|
      545 +|  # Genesis-source availability check (LOCAL node only, connected socket only).
      546 +|  # Bumps the monotonic `:source_check_seq` BEFORE spawning (captured into the
      547 +|  # message, so only the latest in-flight result applies), flags the loading
      548 +|  # window, and spawns the guarded runner on EvoDash.TaskSupervisor — the
      549 +|  # LiveView never blocks on the filesystem probe. Returns the socket.
      550 +|  #
      551 +|  # `node()` cannot be called from a guard, so the local-node test reuses the
      552 +|  # shared `SourceGate.visible?/1` predicate (`current_node in [nil, node()]`,
      553 +|  # mirroring EvoDashWeb.SystemLive.SourceCard.visible?/1).
      554 +|  defp maybe_check_source(socket) do
      555 +|    if connected?(socket) and SourceGate.visible?(socket.assigns[:current_node]) do
      556 +|      seq = socket.assigns[:source_check_seq] + 1
      557 +|      node = socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()
      558 +|
      559 +|      SourceGate.spawn_availability_check(self(), seq, node)
      560 +|
      561 +|      assign(socket, source_check_seq: seq, source_check_loading: true)
      562 +|    else
      563 +|      socket
      564 +|    end
      565 +|  end
      566 +|
      567 +|  @impl true
      568 +|  def handle_params(params, _url, socket) do
      569 +|    prev_node = socket.assigns[:current_node]
      570 +|    prev_node_id = socket.assigns[:current_node_id]
      571 +|    socket = NodeAware.assign_node(socket, params)
      572 +|
      573 +|    # Node switch: the old chat's task belongs to the old node. Start a NEW
      574 +|    # persisted chat (the old one stays in memory under ChatHistory; its
      575 +|    # events are node-filtered anyway) and reset all live chat state.
      576 +|    #
      577 +|    # Two switch shapes:
      578 +|    #   * mid-session switch — `prev_node` was already resolved and changed;
      579 +|    #   * fresh load on a different node — the restored chat carries the node
      580 +|    #     it was created on (`:chat_node`); a mismatch means the chat is stale
      581 +|    #     for the viewed node.
      582 +|    # The first handle_params resolves the mount seed, not a user switch.
      583 +|    # Preserve a matching restored scope there; later target-id changes reset
      584 +|    # even if both targets currently share the same fallback BEAM node.
      585 +|    node_changed =
      586 +|      socket.assigns.model_selection_resolved and
      587 +|        (socket.assigns[:current_node] != prev_node or
      588 +|           socket.assigns[:current_node_id] != prev_node_id)
      589 +|
      590 +|    chat_node_mismatch =
      591 +|      socket.assigns[:chat_node] != nil and
      592 +|        socket.assigns[:chat_node] != socket.assigns[:current_node]
      593 +|
      594 +|    scope = selection_scope(socket)
      595 +|    saved_selection = socket.assigns.model_selection_restore || socket.assigns
      596 +|
      597 +|    saved_scope =
      598 +|      if is_map(saved_selection), do: Map.get(saved_selection, :model_selection_scope)
      599 +|
      600 +|    selection_scope_mismatch = not is_nil(saved_scope) and saved_scope != scope
      601 +|
      602 +|    socket =
      603 +|      socket
      604 +|      |> assign(ChatState.restore_selection(saved_selection, scope))
      605 +|      |> assign(model_selection_restore: nil, model_selection_resolved: true)
      606 +|
      607 +|    socket =
      608 +|      cond do
      609 +|        node_changed or chat_node_mismatch or selection_scope_mismatch ->
      610 +|          start_new_chat(socket)
      611 +|
      612 +|        true ->
      613 +|          socket
      614 +|      end
      615 +|
      616 +|    # Re-load the model profiles / selection-script state for the RESOLVED
      617 +|    # node (a `?node=` switch may have landed on a different target).
      618 +|    socket =
      619 +|      socket
      620 +|      |> assign_model_select()
      621 +|      |> assign(current_path: ~p"/help")
      622 +|      |> assign_recent_projects()
      623 +|      # Genesis-source availability for the resolved node context: fires on the
      624 +|      # connected mount AND on every node-context change (this is the same place
      625 +|      # the page reacts to `?node=` switches). Local-node only + connected-only
      626 +|      # (a dead render must not spawn a task that sends into a dying process).
      627 +|      |> maybe_check_source()
      628 +|      # Subscriptions and restoration are complete, and the viewed node is
      629 +|      # resolved. This also reconciles reconnects routed here by NodeAware.
      630 +|      |> maybe_recover_approvals()
      631 +|
      632 +|    {:noreply, socket}
      633 +|  end
      634 +|
      635 +|  @impl true
      636 +|  def handle_event("send_message", %{"message" => text}, socket), do: send_chat(socket, text)
      637 +|
      638 +|  @impl true
      639 +|  def handle_event("send_message", _params, socket),
      640 +|    do: send_chat(socket, socket.assigns[:chat_draft])
      641 +|
      642 +|  @impl true
      643 +|  def handle_event("chat_input", %{"message" => text}, socket) do
      644 +|    {:noreply, assign(socket, chat_draft: text || "")}
      645 +|  end
      646 +|
      647 +|  @impl true
      648 +|  def handle_event("chat_keydown", %{"key" => key}, socket) do
      649 +|    case key do
      650 +|      "Shift" ->
      651 +|        {:noreply, assign(socket, shift_down: true)}
      652 +|
      653 +|      "Enter" ->
      654 +|        if socket.assigns[:shift_down] do
      655 +|          # Shift held — let the browser insert the newline; do not submit.
      656 +|          {:noreply, socket}
      657 +|        else
      658 +|          send_chat(socket, socket.assigns[:chat_draft])
      659 +|        end
      660 +|
      661 +|      _ ->
      662 +|        {:noreply, socket}
      663 +|    end
      664 +|  end
      665 +|
      666 +|  @impl true
      667 +|  def handle_event("chat_keyup", %{"key" => key}, socket) do
      668 +|    case key do
      669 +|      "Shift" -> {:noreply, assign(socket, shift_down: false)}
      670 +|      _ -> {:noreply, socket}
      671 +|    end
      672 +|  end
      673 +|
      674 +|  @impl true
      675 +|  def handle_event("select_provider_profile", %{"provider_profile_id" => id}, socket) do
      676 +|    update_model_selection(socket, :select_provider, id)
      677 +|  end
      678 +|
      679 +|  @impl true
      680 +|  def handle_event("select_model", %{"model" => model}, socket) do
      681 +|    update_model_selection(socket, :select_model, model)
      682 +|  end
      683 +|
      684 +|  @impl true
      685 +|  def handle_event("select_reasoning_effort", %{"reasoning_effort" => index}, socket) do
      686 +|    update_model_selection(socket, :select_reasoning_effort, index)
      687 +|  end
      688 +|
      689 +|  # Per-message raw/markdown toggle on an assistant entry (phx-click from the
      690 +|  # AssistantMessage hover action group). The entry id is a generated binary —
      691 +|  # used only as a MapSet key, no atom conversion. Ephemeral UI state: kept in
      692 +|  # :raw_entry_ids, never persisted.
      693 +|  @impl true
      694 +|  def handle_event("toggle_assistant_raw", %{"id" => id}, socket) when is_binary(id) do
      695 +|    raw = socket.assigns[:raw_entry_ids] || MapSet.new()
      696 +|
      697 +|    raw =
      698 +|      if MapSet.member?(raw, id) do
      699 +|        MapSet.delete(raw, id)
      700 +|      else
      701 +|        MapSet.put(raw, id)
      702 +|      end
      703 +|
      704 +|    {:noreply, assign(socket, raw_entry_ids: raw)}
      705 +|  end
      706 +|
      707 +|  def handle_event("toggle_assistant_raw", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}
      708 +|
      709 +|  # Fired by the global ClipboardCopy JS hook after a successful copy (same
      710 +|  # flash pattern as tasks_live.ex / review_live.ex).
      711 +|  @impl true
      712 +|  def handle_event("copied", _params, socket) do
      713 +|    {:noreply, put_flash(socket, :info, gettext("Copied to clipboard"))}
      714 +|  end
      715 +|
      716 +|  # Genesis-source gate: "Download source" on the gate banner. No-op while a
      717 +|  # download is already in flight; otherwise mark `source_busy` and spawn the
      718 +|  # clone on EvoDash.TaskSupervisor (the runner seam is resolved AT SPAWN TIME
      719 +|  # inside SourceGate.spawn_clone/2). The result arrives as an async message —
      720 +|  # see the {:source_clone_result, node, result} handle_info clause.
      721 +|  @impl true
      722 +|  def handle_event("download_source", _params, socket) do
      723 +|    if socket.assigns[:source_busy] != nil do
      724 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      725 +|    else
      726 +|      SourceGate.spawn_clone(self(), socket.assigns[:current_node] || node())
      727 +|      {:noreply, assign(socket, source_busy: :clone)}
      728 +|    end
      729 +|  end
      730 +|
      731 +|  # Workspace selection: assign + persist only. Never restarts the running
      732 +|  # task and never clears the transcript.
      733 +|  @impl true
      734 +|  def handle_event("select_workspace", %{"workspace" => path}, socket) do
      735 +|    workspace = if is_binary(path) and path != "", do: path
      736 +|
      737 +|    {:noreply, socket |> assign(workspace: workspace) |> persist_state()}
      738 +|  end
      739 +|
      740 +|  def handle_event("new_chat", _params, socket) do
      741 +|    if socket.assigns[:chat_status] == :idle do
      742 +|      {:noreply, start_new_chat(socket)}
      743 +|    else
      744 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      745 +|    end
      746 +|  end
      747 +|
      748 +|  @impl true
      749 +|  def handle_event("stop", _params, socket) do
      750 +|    case socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] do
      751 +|      nil ->
      752 +|        {:noreply, socket}
      753 +|
      754 +|      task_id ->
      755 +|        # Graceful cancel: :pending → immediate :cancelled; :running →
      756 +|        # :cancelling (agents save + exit, result preserved). The :cancelled
      757 +|        # task event completes the transition and finalizes the transcript.
      758 +|        #
      759 +|        # Guarded per-click mutation: `EvoDash.SafeCall.run/1` converts a store
      760 +|        # failure / cross-node `:erpc` exit (a call timeout to an unreachable
      761 +|        # node EXITS the caller) into an error value, so a dead node flashes
      762 +|        # instead of crashing the page.
      763 +|        result =
      764 +|          EvoDash.SafeCall.run(fn ->
      765 +|            EvoDash.NodeContext.cancel_task(socket.assigns[:current_node] || node(), task_id)
      766 +|          end)
      767 +|
      768 +|        case result do
      769 +|          {:ok, :ok} ->
      770 +|            {:noreply,
      771 +|             socket
      772 +|             |> assign(chat_status: :cancelling, chat_task_status: :cancelling)
      773 +|             |> persist_state()}
      774 +|
      775 +|          {:ok, {:error, reason}} ->
      776 +|            {:noreply,
      777 +|             put_flash(
      778 +|               socket,
      779 +|               :error,
      780 +|               gettext("Failed to stop: %{reason}", reason: inspect(reason))
      781 +|             )}
      782 +|
      783 +|          {:error, reason} ->
      784 +|            {:noreply,
      785 +|             put_flash(
      786 +|               socket,
      787 +|               :error,
      788 +|               gettext("Failed to stop: %{reason}", reason: inspect(reason))
      789 +|             )}
      790 +|
      791 +|          {:ok, _other} ->
      792 +|            # Any other non-error return is still a successful cancel request.
      793 +|            {:noreply,
      794 +|             socket
      795 +|             |> assign(chat_status: :cancelling, chat_task_status: :cancelling)
      796 +|             |> persist_state()}
      797 +|        end
      798 +|    end
      799 +|  end
      800 +|
      801 +|  # Both evaluator and legacy command decisions use the same scoped async
      802 +|  # workflow. Keep the card busy until success; failures retain it for retry.
      803 +|  @impl true
      804 +|  def handle_event(
      805 +|        "approval_response",
      806 +|        %{"request_id" => request_id, "decision" => decision},
      807 +|        socket
      808 +|      ) do
      809 +|    {:noreply, ApprovalWorkflow.respond(socket, request_id, decision)}
      810 +|  end
      811 +|

      813 +|  def handle_event("retry_approvals", _params, socket) do
      814 +|    {:noreply, recover_approvals(socket)}
      815 +|  end
      816 +|
      817 +|  # Missing params / invalid decision (or a blank request id) — no-op.
      818 +|  @impl true
      819 +|  def handle_event("approval_response", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}
      820 +|
      821 +|  @impl true
      822 +|  def handle_info({:task_updated, task_id, status, node} = msg, socket) do
      823 +|    # NodeAware.handle_task_info/2 applies the node filter (drops foreign-node
      824 +|    # events BEFORE the debounce) and schedules the 300ms debounced
      825 +|    # :node_aware_reload_tasks; it returns {:noreply, socket}. We extract the
      826 +|    # socket and then run the chat-specific handling for our own task.
      827 +|    {:noreply, socket} = NodeAware.handle_task_info(socket, msg)
      828 +|    {:noreply, handle_task_event(socket, task_id, status, node)}
      829 +|  end
      830 +|
      831 +|  @impl true
      832 +|  def handle_info({:task_deleted, task_id, node} = msg, socket) do
      833 +|    {:noreply, socket} = NodeAware.handle_task_info(socket, msg)
      834 +|    {:noreply, handle_task_deleted(socket, task_id, node)}
      835 +|  end
      836 +|
      837 +|  @impl true
      838 +|  def handle_info({:agent_registered, id, summary, node}, socket) do
      839 +|    if NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) and
      840 +|         Map.get(summary, :task_id) == socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] do
      841 +|      socket =
      842 +|        assign(socket,
      843 +|          chat_agent_id: id,
      844 +|          agent_message_count: AgentStream.message_count(summary)
      845 +|        )
      846 +|
      847 +|      {:noreply, socket |> persist_state() |> async_fetch_history() |> maybe_recover_approvals()}
      848 +|    else
      849 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      850 +|    end
      851 +|  end
      852 +|
      853 +|  @impl true
      854 +|  def handle_info({:agent_updated, id, changed_fields, node}, socket) do
      855 +|    # `changed_fields` is a KEYWORD LIST in real core broadcasts (e.g.
      856 +|    # `[message_count: n, ...]` from `EvoGit.AgentScheduler.Store.put_agent_state`),
      857 +|    # so membership must be tested with `Keyword.has_key?/2` — the same
      858 +|    # pattern AgentsLive uses (agents_live.ex). The core guarantees
      859 +|    # `:message_count` in changed_fields whenever the agent's context changed;
      860 +|    # no other field change triggers a refetch (HistoryGate choice: refetch
      861 +|    # only when the conversation actually moved).
      862 +|    if NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) and
      863 +|         id == socket.assigns[:chat_agent_id] and
      864 +|         is_list(changed_fields) and Keyword.has_key?(changed_fields, :message_count) do
      865 +|      {:noreply, async_fetch_history(socket)}
      866 +|    else
      867 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      868 +|    end
      869 +|  end
      870 +|
      871 +|  @impl true
      872 +|  def handle_info({:agent_removed, _id, _node}, socket) do
      873 +|    # The terminal task event (task_updated :completed/:cancelled/:failed or
      874 +|    # task_deleted) finalizes the transcript — no action needed here.
      875 +|    {:noreply, socket}
      876 +|  end
      877 +|
      878 +|  @impl true
      879 +|  def handle_info({:agents_updated, node}, socket) do
      880 +|    # Fallback when an agent_registered broadcast was missed: if a task is
      881 +|    # running but no agent is known yet, re-look it up.
      882 +|    if NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) and
      883 +|         socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] != nil and socket.assigns[:chat_agent_id] == nil do
      884 +|      {:noreply, async_lookup_agent(socket)}
      885 +|    else
      886 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      887 +|    end
      888 +|  end
      889 +|
      890 +|  # The workflow applies chat/source-node ownership and snapshot race guards
      891 +|  # for evaluator requests and legacy command approvals alike.
      892 +|  @impl true
      893 +|  def handle_info({:approval_requested, request}, socket) do
      894 +|    {:noreply, ApprovalWorkflow.requested(socket, request)}
      895 +|  end
