      899 +|    {:noreply, ApprovalWorkflow.resolved(socket, request_id, source_node)}
      900 +|  end
      901 +|
      902 +|  @impl true
      903 +|  def handle_info({:approval_resolved, request_id, _decision}, socket) do
      904 +|    {:noreply, ApprovalWorkflow.resolved(socket, request_id)}
      905 +|  end
      906 +|
      907 +|  @impl true
      908 +|  def handle_info(:node_aware_reload_tasks, socket) do
      909 +|    socket =
      910 +|      socket
      911 +|      |> NodeAware.reload_tasks()
      912 +|      |> NodeAware.clear_task_reload_pending()
      913 +|
      914 +|    {:noreply, socket}
      915 +|  end
      916 +|
      917 +|  @impl true
      918 +|  def handle_info({:chat_agent_lookup, node, seq, task_id, agent}, socket) do
      919 +|    if stale?(socket, node, seq) or task_id != socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] do
      920 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      921 +|    else
      922 +|      if is_map(agent) do
      923 +|        socket =
      924 +|          assign(socket,
      925 +|            chat_agent_id: Map.get(agent, :id),
      926 +|            agent_message_count: AgentStream.message_count(agent)
      927 +|          )
      928 +|
      929 +|        {:noreply,
      930 +|         socket |> persist_state() |> async_fetch_history() |> maybe_recover_approvals()}
      931 +|      else
      932 +|        # Agent not registered yet — wait for agent_registered / agents_updated
      933 +|        # to retrigger the lookup.
      934 +|        {:noreply, socket}
      935 +|      end
      936 +|    end
      937 +|  end
      938 +|
      939 +|  @impl true
      940 +|  def handle_info({:chat_history_loaded, node, seq, agent_id, result}, socket) do
      941 +|    if stale?(socket, node, seq) or agent_id != socket.assigns[:chat_agent_id] do
      942 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      943 +|    else
      944 +|      case result do
      945 +|        msgs when is_list(msgs) ->
      946 +|          text = Messages.assistant_text(msgs)
      947 +|
      948 +|          transcript =
      949 +|            if text == "" do
      950 +|              socket.assigns[:transcript]
      951 +|            else
      952 +|              Transcript.put_streaming_text(socket.assigns[:transcript], text)
      953 +|            end
      954 +|
      955 +|          {:noreply,
      956 +|           socket
      957 +|           |> assign(
      958 +|             transcript: transcript,
      959 +|             agent_message_count: length(msgs),
      960 +|             thought_process: Messages.to_entries(msgs)
      961 +|           )
      962 +|           |> persist_state()}
      963 +|
      964 +|        _ ->
      965 +|          {:noreply, socket}
      966 +|      end
      967 +|    end
      968 +|  end
      969 +|
      970 +|  @impl true
      971 +|  def handle_info({:chat_task_loaded, node, seq, task_id, task}, socket) do
      972 +|    if node != socket.assigns[:current_node] or
      973 +|         seq != socket.assigns[:chat_task_fetch_seq] or
      974 +|         task_id != socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] do
      975 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      976 +|    else
      977 +|      {:noreply, finalize_terminal(socket, task)}
      978 +|    end
      979 +|  end
      980 +|
      981 +|  # Genesis-source availability result (from SourceGate.spawn_availability_check/3).
      982 +|  # Stale-guarded: applies only when no newer check was spawned (source_check_seq)
      983 +|  # AND the node the check ran for is still the viewed node. `{:unavailable, _}`
      984 +|  # is treated as "unknown" by the gate (never blocks). TOTAL — any result shape
      985 +|  # is stored verbatim, so a stray message can never leak or crash the page.
      986 +|  @impl true
      987 +|  def handle_info({:source_availability_loaded, seq, node, result}, socket) do
      988 +|    if seq == socket.assigns[:source_check_seq] and is_atom(node) and
      989 +|         NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) do
      990 +|      {:noreply, assign(socket, source_available: result, source_check_loading: false)}
      991 +|    else
      992 +|      {:noreply, socket}
      993 +|    end
      994 +|  end
      995 +|
      996 +|  # Genesis-source download result (from SourceGate.spawn_clone/2). Node-filtered
      997 +|  # and TOTAL: `{:ok, _}` clears the busy marker + re-runs the availability check
      998 +|  # (the gate clears on success); `{:error, reason}` clears it + surfaces the
      999 +|  # failure; `{:unavailable, _}` clears it and resets the gate to "unknown" with
     1000 +|  # NO error flash (a backend the user cannot act on); any other shape is a
     1001 +|  # quiet no-op that still clears the busy marker.
     1002 +|  @impl true
     1003 +|  def handle_info({:source_clone_result, node, result}, socket) do
     1004 +|    if is_atom(node) and NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) do
     1005 +|      case result do
     1006 +|        {:ok, _status} ->
     1007 +|          {:noreply, socket |> assign(source_busy: nil) |> maybe_check_source()}
     1008 +|
     1009 +|        {:error, reason} ->
     1010 +|          {:noreply,
     1011 +|           socket
     1012 +|           |> assign(source_busy: nil)
     1013 +|           |> put_flash(
     1014 +|             :error,
     1015 +|             gettext("Failed to download the Genesis source: %{reason}",
     1016 +|               reason: inspect(reason)
     1017 +|             )
     1018 +|           )}
     1019 +|
     1020 +|        {:unavailable, _reason} ->
     1021 +|          {:noreply, assign(socket, source_busy: nil, source_available: nil)}
     1022 +|
     1023 +|        _other ->
     1024 +|          {:noreply, assign(socket, source_busy: nil)}
     1025 +|      end
     1026 +|    else
     1027 +|      {:noreply, socket}
     1028 +|    end
     1029 +|  end
     1030 +|
     1031 +|  # SafeCall preserves tuple tags as ONE nested element. The workflow rejects
     1032 +|  # stale scopes/tokens and keeps cards on recovery or response failures.
     1033 +|  @impl true
     1034 +|  def handle_info({{:approval_pending_loaded, _seq, _scope} = tag, result}, socket) do
     1035 +|    {:noreply, ApprovalWorkflow.recovered(socket, tag, result)}
     1036 +|  end
     1037 +|
     1038 +|  @impl true
     1039 +|  def handle_info({{:approval_workflow_response, _seq, _scope, _id} = tag, result}, socket) do
     1040 +|    {:noreply, ApprovalWorkflow.responded(socket, tag, result)}
     1041 +|  end
     1042 +|
     1043 +|  # SafeCall keeps the ModelSelect result nested inside its own tagged result.
     1044 +|  # Sequence equality also rejects a stale result for a different target id
     1045 +|  # whose resolved BEAM node happens to be the same.
     1046 +|  @impl true
     1047 +|  def handle_info({{:model_select_loaded, seq, target_node}, result}, socket) do
     1048 +|    if target_node != elem(selection_scope(socket), 1) or
     1049 +|         seq != socket.assigns.model_select_seq or pending_remote?(socket) do
     1050 +|      {:noreply, socket}
     1051 +|    else
     1052 +|      case result do
     1053 +|        {:ok, snapshot_result} ->
     1054 +|          {:noreply, apply_model_snapshot(socket, snapshot_result)}
     1055 +|
     1056 +|        {:error, reason} ->
     1057 +|          {:noreply, apply_model_snapshot(socket, {:error, reason})}
     1058 +|      end
     1059 +|    end
     1060 +|  end
     1061 +|
     1062 +|  @impl true
     1063 +|  # Async, node-aware recent-projects load for the workspace selector.
     1064 +|  # Applies only when the viewed node AND the monotonic sequence still match.
     1065 +|  def handle_info({{:recent_projects_loaded, seq, target_node}, result}, socket) do
     1066 +|    if target_node == (socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()) and
     1067 +|         seq == socket.assigns[:recent_projects_seq] do
     1068 +|      projects =
     1069 +|        case result do
     1070 +|          {:ok, list} -> normalize_projects(list)
     1071 +|          _other -> []
     1072 +|        end
     1073 +|
     1074 +|      workspace =
     1075 +|        socket.assigns[:workspace] ||
     1076 +|          case projects do
     1077 +|            [%{path: path} | _] -> path
     1078 +|            [] -> nil
     1079 +|          end
     1080 +|
     1081 +|      {:noreply,
     1082 +|       socket
     1083 +|       |> assign(recent_projects: projects, workspace: workspace)
     1084 +|       |> persist_state()}
     1085 +|    else
     1086 +|      {:noreply, socket}
     1087 +|    end
     1088 +|  end
     1089 +|
     1090 +|  def handle_info({:node_selected, node_id}, socket) do
     1091 +|    NodeAware.handle_node_selected(socket, node_id)
     1092 +|  end
     1093 +|
     1094 +|  @impl true
     1095 +|  def handle_info({:remote_connection_status, target_id, status}, socket) do
     1096 +|    NodeAware.handle_connection_status(socket, {:remote_connection_status, target_id, status})
     1097 +|  end
     1098 +|
     1099 +|  @impl true
     1100 +|  def handle_info(_msg, socket) do
     1101 +|    # Catch-all — NodeAware's attached hook passes other messages through.
     1102 +|    {:noreply, socket}
     1103 +|  end
     1104 +|
     1105 +|  # Shared submit path for the Send button and the Enter key. Returns
     1106 +|  # {:noreply, socket}.
     1107 +|  #
     1108 +|  # Genesis-source gate: when the GENESIS source is known to be missing on a
     1109 +|  # LOCAL node, a send would only start a doomed `:reflect` task — block it
     1110 +|  # (no optimistic bubbles, no start_task, no seq bump) and point the user at
     1111 +|  # the Download button. Only a KNOWN `false` blocks (see
     1112 +|  # SourceGate.blocked?/1); the textarea/Send button are disabled in the same
     1113 +|  # state, so this is the defensive backstop for the Enter-key path.
     1114 +|  defp send_chat(socket, text) do
     1115 +|    cond do
     1116 +|      pending_remote?(socket) ->
     1117 +|        {:noreply,
     1118 +|         put_flash(
     1119 +|           socket,
     1120 +|           :error,
     1121 +|           gettext("Connect to the remote node before sending a message.")
     1122 +|         )}
     1123 +|
     1124 +|      is_nil(socket.assigns[:workspace]) ->
     1125 +|        {:noreply,
     1126 +|         put_flash(socket, :error, gettext("Select a workspace before sending a message."))}
     1127 +|
     1128 +|      SourceGate.blocked?(socket.assigns) ->
     1129 +|        {:noreply,
     1130 +|         put_flash(
     1131 +|           socket,
     1132 +|           :error,
     1133 +|           gettext("Download the Genesis source before sending a message.")
     1134 +|         )}
     1135 +|
     1136 +|      true ->
     1137 +|        do_send_chat(socket, text)
     1138 +|    end
     1139 +|  end
     1140 +|
     1141 +|  defp do_send_chat(socket, text) do
     1142 +|    if socket.assigns[:chat_status] != :idle do
     1143 +|      # Defensive — the input is disabled while running anyway.
     1144 +|      {:noreply, socket}
     1145 +|    else
     1146 +|      text = String.trim(text || "")
     1147 +|
     1148 +|      if text == "" do
     1149 +|        {:noreply, socket}
     1150 +|      else
     1151 +|        preamble =
     1152 +|          case Transcript.build_preamble(socket.assigns[:transcript]) do
     1153 +|            {:ok, preamble} -> preamble
     1154 +|            :empty -> ""
     1155 +|          end
     1156 +|
     1157 +|        # The workspace block goes FIRST so the agent always knows which
     1158 +|        # directory the conversation is about.
     1159 +|        objective =
     1160 +|          "## Workspace\npath: " <>
     1161 +|            to_string(socket.assigns[:workspace]) <> "\n\n" <> preamble <> "New message: " <> text
     1162 +|
     1163 +|        # Validate before touching the transcript/draft. A missing, stale or
     1164 +|        # wrong-node snapshot must not create a bubble or silently submit Auto.
     1165 +|        case ModelSelect.task_opts(
     1166 +|               socket.assigns.model_snapshot,
     1167 +|               socket.assigns,
     1168 +|               selection_scope(socket),
     1169 +|               mode: "reflect",
     1170 +|               objective: objective
     1171 +|             ) do
     1172 +|          {:ok, task_opts} ->
     1173 +|            start_chat(socket, text, task_opts)
     1174 +|
     1175 +|          {:error, _reason} ->
     1176 +|            {:noreply,
     1177 +|             put_flash(socket, :error, gettext("Choose a valid model before sending a message."))}
     1178 +|        end
     1179 +|      end
     1180 +|    end
     1181 +|  end
     1182 +|
     1183 +|  defp start_chat(socket, text, task_opts) do
     1184 +|    # Optimistic bubbles: the user message + an empty streaming assistant
     1185 +|    # bubble (pulsing dots) appear immediately.
     1186 +|    transcript =
     1187 +|      socket.assigns[:transcript]
     1188 +|      |> Transcript.append(Transcript.entry(:user, text))
     1189 +|      |> Transcript.put_streaming_text("")
     1190 +|
     1191 +|    socket =
     1192 +|      assign(socket,
     1193 +|        transcript: transcript,
     1194 +|        chat_draft: "",
     1195 +|        chat_status: :running,
     1196 +|        chat_node: socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()
     1197 +|      )
     1198 +|
     1199 +|    # Guarded per-click mutation on the viewed node. Options were validated
     1200 +|    # against its ephemeral snapshot before the optimistic transcript update.
     1201 +|    # No :path or legacy :model_id is submitted.
     1202 +|    # `EvoDash.SafeCall.run/1` normalizes the store/RPC outcome: an
     1203 +|    # unreachable remote node EXITS its caller (an `:erpc` failure a rescue
     1204 +|    # cannot catch), so it becomes `{:error, reason}` and lands on the
     1205 +|    # "Failed to start the task" bubble instead of crashing the page.
     1206 +|    result =
     1207 +|      case EvoDash.SafeCall.run(fn ->
     1208 +|             EvoDash.NodeContext.start_task(
     1209 +|               socket.assigns[:current_node] || node(),
     1210 +|               :reflect,
     1211 +|               task_opts
     1212 +|             )
     1213 +|           end) do
     1214 +|        {:ok, start_result} -> start_result
     1215 +|        {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
     1216 +|      end
     1217 +|
     1218 +|    case result do
     1219 +|      {:ok, _task} ->
     1220 +|        case AgentStream.task_id_from_start(result) do
     1221 +|          {:ok, id} ->
     1222 +|            socket =
     1223 +|              socket
     1224 +|              |> assign(chat_task_id: id, chat_task_status: :pending)
     1225 +|              |> persist_state()
     1226 +|              |> async_lookup_agent()
     1227 +|              |> maybe_recover_approvals()
     1228 +|
     1229 +|            {:noreply, socket}
     1230 +|
     1231 +|          {:error, :no_task_id} ->
     1232 +|            socket =
     1233 +|              socket
     1234 +|              |> finalize_streaming(gettext("Failed to start the task: no task id returned"))
     1235 +|              |> assign(:chat_status, :idle)
     1236 +|              |> persist_state()
     1237 +|
     1238 +|            {:noreply, socket}
     1239 +|        end
     1240 +|
     1241 +|      {:error, reason} ->
     1242 +|        socket =
     1243 +|          socket
     1244 +|          |> finalize_streaming(
     1245 +|            gettext("Failed to start the task: %{reason}", reason: inspect(reason))
     1246 +|          )
     1247 +|          |> assign(:chat_status, :idle)
     1248 +|          |> persist_state()
     1249 +|
     1250 +|        {:noreply, socket}
     1251 +|    end
     1252 +|  end
     1253 +|
     1254 +|  # Spawns a supervised lookup of the reflect task's root agent. `node_override`
     1255 +|  # targets a specific node (mount reconciliation uses the chat's OWN
     1256 +|  # `:chat_node`); the default is the viewed node. Bumps :chat_fetch_seq so
     1257 +|  # only the latest in-flight result applies.
     1258 +|  #
     1259 +|  # The RPC runs through `EvoDash.SafeCall.run/2` INSIDE the task: an `:erpc`
     1260 +|  # to a remote node EXITS the caller (a rescue cannot catch an exit), and a
     1261 +|  # dying child would never send its result message — the lookup would be lost.
     1262 +|  # Normalized to `[]` on any error, so the caller simply waits for the
     1263 +|  # agent_registered / agents_updated retrigger.
     1264 +|  defp async_lookup_agent(socket, node_override \\ nil) do
     1265 +|    task_id = socket.assigns[:chat_task_id]
     1266 +|    node = node_override || socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()
     1267 +|    seq = socket.assigns[:chat_fetch_seq] + 1
     1268 +|    pid = self()
     1269 +|
     1270 +|    Task.Supervisor.start_child(EvoDash.TaskSupervisor, fn ->
     1271 +|      agents = EvoDash.SafeCall.run(fn -> EvoDash.NodeContext.list_agents(node) end, [])
     1272 +|      agent = AgentStream.find_root_agent(agents, task_id)
     1273 +|      send(pid, {:chat_agent_lookup, node, seq, task_id, agent})
     1274 +|    end)
     1275 +|
     1276 +|    assign(socket, chat_fetch_seq: seq)
     1277 +|  end
     1278 +|
     1279 +|  # Spawns a supervised history fetch for the chat agent. `node_override`
     1280 +|  # targets a specific node (see async_lookup_agent/2). Bumps :chat_fetch_seq
     1281 +|  # (HistoryGate-style: only refetched when the agent's message_count moved).
     1282 +|  # Same guarded-task contract: any call failure degrades to `[]` (the
     1283 +|  # handler's unchanged branch) and the result message is always sent.
     1284 +|  defp async_fetch_history(socket, node_override \\ nil) do
     1285 +|    agent_id = socket.assigns[:chat_agent_id]
     1286 +|    node = node_override || socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()
     1287 +|    seq = socket.assigns[:chat_fetch_seq] + 1
     1288 +|    pid = self()
     1289 +|
     1290 +|    Task.Supervisor.start_child(EvoDash.TaskSupervisor, fn ->
     1291 +|      result =
     1292 +|        EvoDash.SafeCall.run(fn -> EvoDash.NodeContext.get_agent_history(node, agent_id) end, [])
     1293 +|
     1294 +|      send(pid, {:chat_history_loaded, node, seq, agent_id, result})
     1295 +|    end)
     1296 +|
     1297 +|    assign(socket, chat_fetch_seq: seq)
     1298 +|  end
     1299 +|
     1300 +|  # Spawns a supervised task fetch used to finalize the transcript with the
     1301 +|  # preserved/final result (also the ONE-SHOT mount reconciliation fetch).
     1302 +|  # Bumps :chat_task_fetch_seq (its own counter, so a late history/lookup
     1303 +|  # fetch can never invalidate the terminal result). Uses the lite read (no
     1304 +|  # archive blob — the terminal path reads only status/result/error). A
     1305 +|  # failed/timed-out fetch degrades to `nil`, which finalize_terminal/2 treats
     1306 +|  # as deleted.
     1307 +|  defp async_fetch_task(socket, node_override \\ nil) do
     1308 +|    task_id = socket.assigns[:chat_task_id]
     1309 +|    node = node_override || socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()
     1310 +|    seq = socket.assigns[:chat_task_fetch_seq] + 1
     1311 +|    pid = self()
     1312 +|
     1313 +|    Task.Supervisor.start_child(EvoDash.TaskSupervisor, fn ->
     1314 +|      task = EvoDash.SafeCall.run(fn -> EvoDash.NodeContext.get_task_lite(node, task_id) end, nil)
     1315 +|
     1316 +|      send(pid, {:chat_task_loaded, node, seq, task_id, task})
     1317 +|    end)
     1318 +|
     1319 +|    assign(socket, chat_task_fetch_seq: seq)
     1320 +|  end
     1321 +|
     1322 +|  # Chat-specific handling of a task lifecycle event for OUR task (node filter
     1323 +|  # and task_id match already checked by the caller). Every status updates the
     1324 +|  # badge assign (`:chat_task_status`); `nil` = review-only mutation → no-op.
     1325 +|  defp handle_task_event(socket, task_id, status, node) do
     1326 +|    if NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) and
     1327 +|         task_id == socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] do
     1328 +|      socket =
     1329 +|        case status do
     1330 +|          :completed ->
     1331 +|            async_fetch_task(assign(socket, chat_task_status: :completed))
     1332 +|
     1333 +|          :cancelled ->
     1334 +|            async_fetch_task(assign(socket, chat_task_status: :cancelled))
     1335 +|
     1336 +|          :failed ->
     1337 +|            socket
     1338 +|            |> assign(chat_task_status: :failed)
     1339 +|            |> finalize_streaming(gettext("The task failed."))
     1340 +|            |> clear_task_refs()
     1341 +|
     1342 +|          :running ->
     1343 +|            socket = assign(socket, chat_task_status: :running)
     1344 +|
     1345 +|            if socket.assigns[:chat_agent_id] == nil do
     1346 +|              async_lookup_agent(socket)
     1347 +|            else
     1348 +|              socket
     1349 +|            end
     1350 +|
     1351 +|          :cancelling ->
     1352 +|            assign(socket, chat_task_status: :cancelling, chat_status: :cancelling)
     1353 +|
     1354 +|          :pending ->
     1355 +|            assign(socket, chat_task_status: :pending)
     1356 +|
     1357 +|          :finalizing ->
     1358 +|            assign(socket, chat_task_status: :finalizing)
     1359 +|
     1360 +|          nil ->
     1361 +|            socket
     1362 +|
     1363 +|          _ ->
     1364 +|            socket
     1365 +|        end
     1366 +|
     1367 +|      persist_state(socket)
     1368 +|    else
     1369 +|      socket
     1370 +|    end
     1371 +|  end
     1372 +|
     1373 +|  # The chat task was deleted. Finalize with a marker and clear the task refs
     1374 +|  # (the transcript stays visible so the user sees the history).
     1375 +|  defp handle_task_deleted(socket, task_id, node) do
     1376 +|    if NodeAware.event_from_current_node?(socket.assigns, node) and
     1377 +|         task_id == socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] do
     1378 +|      socket
     1379 +|      |> finalize_streaming(gettext("The conversation was deleted."))
     1380 +|      |> clear_task_refs()
     1381 +|      |> persist_state()
     1382 +|    else
     1383 +|      socket
     1384 +|    end
     1385 +|  end
     1386 +|
     1387 +|  # Finalizes the last streaming assistant bubble (or appends a new assistant
     1388 +|  # entry) with `text`.
     1389 +|  defp finalize_streaming(socket, text) do
     1390 +|    assign(socket, transcript: Transcript.finalize_assistant(socket.assigns[:transcript], text))
     1391 +|  end
     1392 +|
     1393 +|  # Applies a fetched task to the transcript. Terminal statuses finalize the
     1394 +|  # transcript and clear the task/agent refs (the transcript AND the final
     1395 +|  # badge stay visible; New chat is re-enabled). Non-terminal statuses (e.g. a
     1396 +|  # mount-reconciliation fetch that found a still-alive task) only refresh the
     1397 +|  # badge/status assigns — the task is still alive, so the refs MUST stay for
     1398 +|  # further task/agent events to match (no finalization, no clearing).
     1399 +|  defp finalize_terminal(socket, task) do
     1400 +|    cond do
     1401 +|      task == nil ->
     1402 +|        socket
     1403 +|        |> finalize_streaming(gettext("The conversation was deleted."))
     1404 +|        |> clear_task_refs()
     1405 +|        |> persist_state()
     1406 +|
     1407 +|      is_map(task) ->
     1408 +|        case Map.get(task, :status) do
     1409 +|          status when status in @terminal_statuses ->
     1410 +|            socket = assign(socket, chat_task_status: status)
     1411 +|
     1412 +|            socket =
     1413 +|              case status do
     1414 +|                :completed -> finalize_completed(socket, Map.get(task, :result))
     1415 +|                :cancelled -> finalize_cancelled(socket, Map.get(task, :result))
     1416 +|                :failed -> finalize_failed(socket, Map.get(task, :error))
     1417 +|              end
     1418 +|
     1419 +|            socket
     1420 +|            |> clear_task_refs()
     1421 +|            |> persist_state()
     1422 +|
     1423 +|          :cancelling ->
     1424 +|            socket
     1425 +|            |> assign(chat_task_status: :cancelling, chat_status: :cancelling)
     1426 +|            |> persist_state()
     1427 +|
     1428 +|          status when status in [:running, :pending, :finalizing] ->
     1429 +|            socket
     1430 +|            |> assign(chat_task_status: status)
     1431 +|            |> persist_state()
     1432 +|
     1433 +|          _ ->
     1434 +|            # Missing/unknown status on a live task: nothing to apply — keep
     1435 +|            # refs and current state (a later broadcast will finalize).
     1436 +|            socket
     1437 +|        end
     1438 +|
     1439 +|      true ->
     1440 +|        socket
     1441 +|    end
     1442 +|  end
     1443 +|
     1444 +|  defp finalize_completed(socket, result) do
     1445 +|    case AgentStream.extract_final_text(result) do
     1446 +|      {:ok, text} -> finalize_streaming(socket, text)
     1447 +|      :empty -> finalize_streaming(socket, gettext("No response."))
     1448 +|      :error -> finalize_streaming(socket, gettext("The task failed."))
     1449 +|    end
     1450 +|  end
     1451 +|
     1452 +|  defp finalize_cancelled(socket, result) do
     1453 +|    # Graceful cancel preserves the final result when the agent completed.
     1454 +|    case AgentStream.extract_final_text(result) do
     1455 +|      {:ok, text} -> finalize_streaming(socket, text)
     1456 +|      _ -> finalize_streaming(socket, gettext("Stopped."))
     1457 +|    end
     1458 +|  end
     1459 +|
     1460 +|  # Finalizes a :failed task (the fetched-task terminal path). When the decoded
     1461 +|  # TaskInfo carries a structured error payload, its `error.message` (e.g.
     1462 +|  # "Task force-killed by user") is the explanation shown to the user; a nil /
     1463 +|  # non-map / blank error falls back to the generic failure placeholder.
     1464 +|  # Total: AgentStream.extract_failed_message/1 never raises on malformed data.
     1465 +|  defp finalize_failed(socket, error) do
     1466 +|    case AgentStream.extract_failed_message(error) do
     1467 +|      nil -> finalize_streaming(socket, gettext("The task failed."))
     1468 +|      message -> finalize_streaming(socket, message)
     1469 +|    end
     1470 +|  end
     1471 +|
     1472 +|  # Terminal state: drops the task/agent refs (no further task/agent events
     1473 +|  # can match) and re-enables input, keeping the transcript AND the final
     1474 +|  # `:chat_task_status` badge (and thought process) visible on the card.
     1475 +|  defp clear_task_refs(socket) do
     1476 +|    socket
     1477 +|    |> assign(
     1478 +|      chat_task_id: nil,
     1479 +|      chat_agent_id: nil,
     1480 +|      agent_message_count: nil,
     1481 +|      chat_status: :idle
     1482 +|    )
     1483 +|    |> ApprovalWorkflow.init()
     1484 +|  end
     1485 +|
     1486 +|  # Full live-chat reset (New chat / node switch): clears everything including
     1487 +|  # the transcript. The persisted chat (if any) is untouched —
     1488 +|  # `start_new_chat/1` owns creating + persisting the fresh empty chat.
     1489 +|  defp reset_chat(socket) do
     1490 +|    socket
     1491 +|    |> assign(
     1492 +|      transcript: Transcript.new(),
     1493 +|      chat_draft: "",
     1494 +|      chat_status: :idle,
     1495 +|      chat_task_id: nil,
     1496 +|      chat_agent_id: nil,
     1497 +|      agent_message_count: nil,
     1498 +|      chat_task_status: nil,
     1499 +|      thought_process: [],
     1500 +|      chat_node: nil,
     1501 +|      chat_fetch_seq: 0,
     1502 +|      chat_task_fetch_seq: 0,
     1503 +|      # Selection survives New chat only within its resolved target scope;
     1504 +|      # handle_params clears cross-node/target selections before this reset.
     1505 +|      # Per-entry raw-view flags are cleared with their transcript entries.
     1506 +|      raw_entry_ids: MapSet.new(),
     1507 +|      approval_context: nil
     1508 +|    )
     1509 +|    |> ApprovalWorkflow.init()
     1510 +|  end
     1511 +|
     1512 +|  # Compare only chat/node refs, not draft/history changes. A fresh connected
     1513 +|  # mount reconciles once after restoration and node resolution; root lookup,
     1514 +|  # task start and reconnect/node changes reconcile only when their scope moves.
     1515 +|  # The workflow performs the usable-ref and pending-remote checks itself.
     1516 +|  defp maybe_recover_approvals(socket) do
     1517 +|    context =
     1518 +|      Map.take(socket.assigns, [
     1519 +|        :chat_id,
     1520 +|        :current_node_id,
     1521 +|        :current_node,
     1522 +|        :chat_node,
     1523 +|        :chat_task_id,
     1524 +|        :chat_agent_id
     1525 +|      ])
     1526 +|
     1527 +|    if connected?(socket) and context != socket.assigns[:approval_context] do
     1528 +|      socket
     1529 +|      |> assign(approval_context: context)
     1530 +|      |> recover_approvals()
     1531 +|    else
     1532 +|      socket
     1533 +|    end
     1534 +|  end
     1535 +|
     1536 +|  defp recover_approvals(socket) do
     1537 +|    if connected?(socket), do: ApprovalWorkflow.recover(socket), else: socket
     1538 +|  end
     1539 +|
     1540 +|  # Starts a NEW persisted chat (old chats stay in ChatHistory — there is no
     1541 +|  # chat-switching UI yet) and resets all live chat state, persisting the
     1542 +|  # fresh empty state. The store mutation is connected-only: a dead-render
     1543 +|  # handle_params with `?node=` must not create a chat per HTTP request.
     1544 +|  # Prune cap: keep the newest 10 chats (bounded memory).
     1545 +|  defp start_new_chat(socket) do
     1546 +|    socket =
     1547 +|      if connected?(socket) do
     1548 +|        chat_id = EvoDash.ChatHistory.new_chat()
     1549 +|        EvoDash.ChatHistory.prune(10)
     1550 +|        assign(socket, chat_id: chat_id)
     1551 +|      else
     1552 +|        socket
     1553 +|      end
     1554 +|
     1555 +|    socket
     1556 +|    |> reset_chat()
     1557 +|    |> persist_state()
     1558 +|  end
     1559 +|
     1560 +|  # Connected mounts attach to the persisted current chat (creating one when
     1561 +|  # none exists) and restore its state. The dead render deliberately SKIPS the
     1562 +|  # store — the initial HTTP request must not create a chat per page load.
     1563 +|  defp attach_chat(socket) do
     1564 +|    if connected?(socket) do
     1565 +|      chat_id = EvoDash.ChatHistory.current_chat_id() || EvoDash.ChatHistory.new_chat()
     1566 +|
     1567 +|      saved_state = EvoDash.ChatHistory.get_state(chat_id)
     1568 +|
     1569 +|      socket =
     1570 +|        socket
     1571 +|        |> assign(chat_id: chat_id, model_selection_restore: saved_state || %{})
     1572 +|        |> assign(ChatState.restore(saved_state))
     1573 +|
     1574 +|      reconcile_chat(socket)
     1575 +|    else
     1576 +|      socket
     1577 +|    end
     1578 +|  end
     1579 +|
     1580 +|  # ONE-SHOT reconciliation (NOT polling): a chat that was mid-flight when the
     1581 +|  # page was left missed its terminal task events. Re-fetch the task once — a
     1582 +|  # terminal status finalizes the transcript, a live status just refreshes the
     1583 +|  # badge — and re-link the agent stream (a single history refetch, or a
     1584 +|  # lookup when the agent was never observed). All fetches target the chat's
     1585 +|  # OWN node (`:chat_node`), not the viewed node — the chat's task lives there.
     1586 +|  defp reconcile_chat(socket) do
     1587 +|    if socket.assigns[:chat_status] in [:running, :cancelling] and
     1588 +|         socket.assigns[:chat_task_id] != nil do
     1589 +|      socket = async_fetch_task(socket, socket.assigns[:chat_node])
     1590 +|
     1591 +|      if socket.assigns[:chat_agent_id] != nil do
     1592 +|        async_fetch_history(socket, socket.assigns[:chat_node])
     1593 +|      else
     1594 +|        async_lookup_agent(socket, socket.assigns[:chat_node])
     1595 +|      end
     1596 +|    else
     1597 +|      socket
     1598 +|    end
     1599 +|  end
     1600 +|
     1601 +|  # Persists the full chat state into ChatHistory (in-memory, opaque per-chat
     1602 +|  # map — the LiveView owns the shape via ChatState). Skipped on dead renders
     1603 +|  # (no chat attached yet). Persist points: send, stop, every task/agent
     1604 +|  # status event, the async lookup/history/task results, finalize paths, New
     1605 +|  # chat, and node switches. The `chat_input` draft is NOT persisted per
     1606 +|  # keystroke (documented choice).
     1607 +|  defp persist_state(socket) do
     1608 +|    if connected?(socket) and socket.assigns[:chat_id] != nil do
     1609 +|      EvoDash.ChatHistory.put_state(socket.assigns[:chat_id], ChatState.build(socket.assigns))
     1610 +|    end
     1611 +|
     1612 +|    socket
     1613 +|  end
     1614 +|
     1615 +|  # Loads the viewed node's recent projects for the workspace selector, OFF the
     1616 +|  # LiveView process (a remote node answers via `:erpc`). Bumps the monotonic
     1617 +|  # `:recent_projects_seq` guard first; a failed load degrades to `[]` (the
     1618 +|  # selector still offers the "Open or create project" link).
     1619 +|  defp assign_recent_projects(socket) do
     1620 +|    target_node = socket.assigns[:current_node] || node()
     1621 +|    seq = socket.assigns[:recent_projects_seq] + 1
     1622 +|    socket = assign(socket, recent_projects_seq: seq)
     1623 +|
     1624 +|    if connected?(socket) do
     1625 +|      case EvoDash.SafeCall.start(self(), {:recent_projects_loaded, seq, target_node}, fn ->
     1626 +|             EvoDash.NodeContext.list_recent_projects(target_node)
     1627 +|           end) do
     1628 +|        {:ok, _pid} -> socket
     1629 +|        {:error, _reason} -> assign(socket, recent_projects: [])
     1630 +|      end
     1631 +|    else
     1632 +|      socket
     1633 +|    end
     1634 +|  end
     1635 +|
     1636 +|  # Normalizes both struct and string-keyed map projections into
     1637 +|  # `%{path: binary, name: binary}`; malformed entries are dropped.
     1638 +|  defp normalize_projects(projects) when is_list(projects) do
     1639 +|    projects
     1640 +|    |> Enum.map(fn
     1641 +|      %{path: path} = project when is_binary(path) ->
     1642 +|        %{path: path, name: project_name(project) || path}
     1643 +|
     1644 +|      %{"path" => path} = project when is_binary(path) ->
     1645 +|        %{path: path, name: project_name(project) || path}
     1646 +|
     1647 +|      _other ->
     1648 +|        nil
     1649 +|    end)
     1650 +|    |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
     1651 +|  end
     1652 +|
     1653 +|  defp normalize_projects(_projects), do: []
     1654 +|
     1655 +|  defp project_name(project) do
     1656 +|    case Map.get(project, :name) || Map.get(project, "name") do
     1657 +|      name when is_binary(name) and name != "" -> name
     1658 +|      _other -> nil
     1659 +|    end
     1660 +|  end
     1661 +|
     1662 +|  defp workspace_link(nil), do: "/projects"
     1663 +|  defp workspace_link(node_id), do: "/projects?node=" <> to_string(node_id)
     1664 +|
     1665 +|  # Stale-guard for async chat fetches: the result applies only when the node
     1666 +|  # context is unchanged AND no newer fetch was spawned since.
     1667 +|  defp stale?(socket, node, seq) do
     1668 +|    node != socket.assigns[:current_node] or seq != socket.assigns[:chat_fetch_seq]
     1669 +|  end
     1670 +|end
     1671 +|

    422 +|  # Extract a display label for a task: objective > prompt > truncated task ID
    423 +|  defp task_label(task) do
    424 +|    opts = Map.get(task, :opts, []) || []
    425 +|    label = opts[:objective] || opts[:prompt] || String.slice(task.id, 0, 8)
    426 +|
    427 +|    if String.length(label) > 30 do
    428 +|      String.slice(label, 0, 30) <> "..."
    429 +|    else
    430 +|      label
    431 +|    end
    432 +|  end
    433 +|
    434 +|  # Format a DateTime as a relative elapsed string like "2m ago", "1h ago"
    435 +|  defp format_elapsed(nil), do: ""
    436 +|
    437 +|  defp format_elapsed(dt) do
    438 +|    diff = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), dt)
    439 +|
    440 +|    cond do
    441 +|      diff < 30 -> gettext("just now")
    442 +|      diff < 60 -> "#{diff}s ago"
    443 +|      diff < 3600 -> "#{div(diff, 60)}m ago"
    444 +|      diff < 86400 -> "#{div(diff, 3600)}h ago"
    445 +|      true -> "#{div(diff, 86400)}d ago"
    446 +|    end
    447 +|  end
    448 +|
    449 +|  # Groups running and pending tasks by project name for sidebar display.
    450 +|  # Returns a list of {project_name, tasks} tuples sorted alphabetically by
    451 +|  # project name, with unpathed tasks at the top as "Other".
    452 +|  defp group_tasks_by_project(running_tasks, pending_tasks) do
    453 +|    # Defense-in-depth: the sidebar lists are seeded from the shape-agnostic
    454 +|    # ActiveTasks hub, so skip any non-map entry instead of crashing the page.
    455 +|    all = (running_tasks ++ pending_tasks) |> Enum.filter(&is_map/1)
    456 +|
    457 +|    grouped =
    458 +|      Enum.group_by(all, fn task ->
    459 +|        case Map.get(task, :project_path) do
    460 +|          nil -> nil
    461 +|          path -> Path.basename(path)
    462 +|        end
    463 +|      end)
    464 +|
    465 +|    {others, named} = Map.pop(grouped, nil, [])
    466 +|
    467 +|    sorted_named =
    468 +|      Enum.sort(named, fn {a, _}, {b, _} ->
    469 +|        String.downcase(a) <= String.downcase(b)
    470 +|      end)
    471 +|
    472 +|    sorted_groups =
    473 +|      Enum.map(sorted_named, fn {name, tasks} ->
    474 +|        {running, pending} =
    475 +|          Enum.split_with(tasks, &(&1.status in [:running, :pending, :finalizing, :cancelling]))
    476 +|
    477 +|        {name, running ++ pending}
    478 +|      end)
    479 +|
    480 +|    if others != [] do
    481 +|      {running, pending} =
    482 +|        Enum.split_with(others, &(&1.status in [:running, :pending, :finalizing, :cancelling]))
    483 +|
    484 +|      [{"Other", running ++ pending} | sorted_groups]
    485 +|    else
    486 +|      sorted_groups
    487 +|    end
    488 +|  end
    489 +|
